~use-package~ 不写 ~:straight~。

目标清单是本节的核心：5 个文件（~actions~、~work~、~personal~、~routines~、~someday~）配
~:maxlevel . 3~，只能 refile 到三级以内的标题。三处改动让选目标更顺手：
~org-refile-use-outline-path 'file~（默认 nil，补全列表里带上文件名）、
~org-outline-path-complete-in-steps nil~（默认 t，一次补全到底而不是逐层问）、
~org-refile-allow-creating-parent-nodes 'confirm~（默认 nil，可以现场建父节点但要确认）。
~:hook (org-after-refile-insert . save-buffer)~ 让 refile 完立刻落盘——refile 跨文件改写，
不保存就等于没做。注意这份清单与 ~org-agenda-files~、capture 模板里的落点必须同步，新增一个文件
要改三处。

+ 包名（GNU ELPA）：~org-refile~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-straight/org-mode>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
;; [org-refile]
(use-package org-refile
  :hook (org-after-refile-insert . save-buffer)
  :init
  ;; Refile clarified inbox items into an action list or a project.
  (setq org-refile-targets
        `((,(mapcar (lambda (file)
                      (expand-file-name (concat "agenda/" file) org-directory))
                    '("actions.org" "work.org" "personal.org"
                      "routines.org" "someday.org"))
           :maxlevel . 3))
        org-refile-use-outline-path 'file
        org-outline-path-complete-in-steps nil
        org-refile-allow-creating-parent-nodes 'confirm))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-agenda.el= section for ~org-capture~

源文件 Commentary：=org-remember.el= 的替代实现，流程更精简、目标类型更多。~:require-incrementally
(org-agenda t)~ 说明它依赖 agenda 那一批子文件。

9 条模板按「先收后理」组织：~t~/~n~ 进 =inbox.org= 当任务或笔记（都带 ~:CREATED: %U~ 与
~%a~ 回链），~a~ 直接进 =actions.org= 的 Actions 标题下，~p~ 是分组（~pw~ 工作项目、~pp~ 个人项目，
两者都生成「父 TODO + ~** NEXT~ 子项」的两段式），~r~ 是带 ~SCHEDULED~ 与
~:APPT_WARNTIME:~ 的提醒（后者与下一节的 appt 相扣），~R~ 是重复提醒——它用 ~%(...)~ 现场问
「第一次什么时候、间隔多久」，选项就是 Org 的重复语法 ~.+1d~、~++1w~、~++1m~ 等，所以重复语义仍然
是 Org 自己的；~s~ 进 someday，~e~ 进 calendar 当事件。每条都带 ~:empty-lines 1~，连续 capture
时条目之间不会黏在一起。~org-default-notes-file~ 指向 =inbox.org=，因此 ~t~、~n~、~r~ 都不写死路径。

+ 包名（GNU ELPA）：~org-capture~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-straight/org-mode>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
;; [org-capture]
(use-package org-capture
  :require-incrementally (org-agenda t)
  :bind ("C-c o c" . org-capture)
  :config
  (setq
   org-default-notes-file (expand-file-name "agenda/inbox.org" org-directory)
   ;; Capture quickly; clarify and organize during inbox processing.
   org-capture-templates '(("t" "Inbox task" entry
                            (file org-default-notes-file)
                            "* TODO %?\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n%a\n"
                            :empty-lines 1)
                           ("n" "Inbox note" entry
                            (file org-default-notes-file)
                            "* %? :note:\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n%a\n"
                            :empty-lines 1)
                           ("a" "Next action" entry
                            (file+headline "agenda/actions.org" "Actions")
                            "* NEXT %?\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n%a\n"
                            :empty-lines 1)
                           ("p" "Project")
                           ("pw" "Work project" entry
                            (file "agenda/work.org")
                            "* TODO %^{Project name} :project:\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n** NEXT %?\n"
                            :empty-lines 1)
                           ("pp" "Personal project" entry
                            (file "agenda/personal.org")
                            "* TODO %^{Project name} :project:\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n** NEXT %?\n"
                            :empty-lines 1)
                           ("r" "Reminder" entry
                            (file org-default-notes-file)
                            "* TODO %?\nSCHEDULED: %^{When}T\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:APPT_WARNTIME: %^{Warn before (minutes)|15}\n:END:\n"
                            :empty-lines 1)
                           ("R" "Repeating reminder" entry
                            (file+headline "agenda/routines.org" "Recurring")
                            "* TODO %?
SCHEDULED: %(let ((time (org-read-date t t nil \"First occurrence: \")))
              (format \"<%s %s>\"
                      (format-time-string \"%Y-%m-%d %a %H:%M\" time)
                      (completing-read
                       \"Repeat interval: \"
                       '(\".+1d\" \".+1w\" \".+1m\" \"++1w\" \"++1m\" \"++1y\")
                       nil t)))
:PROPERTIES:
:CREATED: %U
:APPT_WARNTIME: %^{Warn before (minutes)|15}
:END:
"
                            :empty-lines 1)
                           ("s" "Someday / maybe" entry
                            (file+headline "agenda/someday.org" "Someday / Maybe")
                            "* SOMEDAY %?\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:END:\n%a\n"
                            :empty-lines 1)
                           ("e" "Calendar event" entry
                            (file "agenda/calendar.org")
                            "* %^{Title}\n:PROPERTIES:\n:CREATED: %U\n:APPT_WARNTIME: %^{Warn before (minutes)|15}\n:END:\n%^{When}T\n%?\n"
                            :empty-lines 1))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-agenda.el= section for ~org-agenda~

源文件 Commentary：创建与使用 Org 的 Agenda。这一节是全部任务流的出口，也是本模块最长的一块。

入口是自定义命令里的 ~d~（Dashboard）：一个 agenda 块 + 「本周到期的习惯」+ 按状态分组的 todo
（Doing/Next/TODO/Waiting/Someday），另有五个单状态命令 ~g~/~n~/~w~/~p~/~s~。两个 skip 函数是它的
配套：~+agenda-skip-habit~ 把重复任务从普通块里排除；~+agenda-skip-unless-habit-due-this-week~
反过来只收「今天到本周日」到期的习惯，区间重复（如 =.+2d/4d=）取最晚那个到期日。两者都靠
~(save-excursion (outline-next-heading) (point))~ 返回跳过终点，这是 ~org-agenda-skip-function~ 的约定。

~+agenda-show-breadcrumbs~ 挂在 ~org-agenda-finalize~ 上，给每行标题后追加「文件名 ▸ 父标题」
（分隔符是 ~org-agenda-breadcrumbs-separator " ▸ "~，默认 ~"->"~）。它要手工插文本属性，所以先检查
这一行是否已经标过（~+agenda-breadcrumbs~ 标记），避免 finalize 跑两遍时叠加；追加完再调
~org-agenda-align-tags~ 把标签对齐（除非 ~org-agenda-remove-tags~ 为 t）。

其余设置按用途读：文件列表 7 个由 ~org-directory~ 推出（~actions/calendar/inbox/personal/
routines/someday/work~）；显示上 ~org-agenda-sticky t~（默认 nil，agenda buffer 保持刷新型）、
~org-agenda-window-setup 'current-window~（默认 ~reorganize-frame~，不重排整个 frame）、
~org-agenda-restore-windows-after-quit t~、~org-agenda-prefix-format~ 让 agenda 行只留时间与
scheduled/deadline、todo/tags/search 块不缩进；过滤上 ~org-agenda-dim-blocked-tasks nil~（默认 t，
阻塞任务不淡化）、~org-agenda-use-tag-inheritance nil~（默认继承，这里关掉以免每条都带父标签）、
~org-agenda-ignore-properties '(stats)~（不做统计，快一些）、~org-agenda-skip-scheduled-if-done~ 与
~org-agenda-skip-deadline-if-done~ 从默认 nil 打开；~org-agenda-deadline-faces~ 按剩余天数分四级。
最后一条 ~:bind~ 里的 remap 指向下一节的 ~+agenda-calendar-blocks~：在 agenda 里按
~org-agenda-goto-calendar~ 得到的是块状周视图。

+ 包名（GNU ELPA）：~org-agenda~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-straight/org-mode>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
;; [org-agenda]
(use-package org-agenda
  :require-incrementally t
  :hook (org-agenda-finalize . +agenda-show-breadcrumbs)
  :bind (("C-c o a" . org-agenda)
         :map org-agenda-mode-map
         ([remap org-agenda-goto-calendar] . +agenda-calendar-blocks))
  :preface
  (defun +agenda-show-breadcrumbs ()
    "Append a muted file and parent path after each agenda item's title."
    (let ((inhibit-read-only t))
      (save-excursion
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (while (not (eobp))
          (let* ((end (line-end-position))
                 (marker (org-get-at-bol 'org-hd-marker))
                 (heading (text-property-any (point) end 'org-heading t)))
            (when (and marker heading
                       (not (text-property-any (point) end '+agenda-breadcrumbs t)))
              (when-let* ((path (org-with-point-at marker
                                 (when-let* ((file (buffer-file-name (buffer-base-buffer))))
                                   (cons (file-name-base file) (org-get-outline-path))))))
                (goto-char heading)
                (if (re-search-forward org-tag-group-re end t)
                    (goto-char (match-beginning 0))
                  (goto-char end))
                (skip-chars-backward " \t" heading)
                (insert (propertize
                         (concat "  " (string-join path org-agenda-breadcrumbs-separator))
                         'face 'font-lock-comment-face
                         'org-heading nil '+agenda-breadcrumbs t)))))
          (forward-line 1)))
      (unless (eq org-agenda-remove-tags t)
        (org-agenda-align-tags))))

  (defun +agenda-skip-habit ()
    "Keep repeating tasks out of ordinary agenda blocks."
    (when (org-get-repeat)
      (save-excursion (outline-next-heading) (point))))

  (defun +agenda-skip-unless-habit-due-this-week ()
    "Keep repeating tasks due from today through Sunday, inclusive."
    (let* ((today (org-today))
           (repeat (org-get-repeat))
           (deadline (org-get-deadline-time (point)))
           (due (cond ((not repeat) nil)
                      (deadline (time-to-days deadline))
                      (t (+ (org-time-string-to-absolute (org-entry-get nil "SCHEDULED"))
                            ;; Use the latest due date of ranges like .+2d/4d.
                            (if (string-match "/" repeat)
                                (- (org-habit-duration-to-days (substring repeat (match-end 0)))
                                   (org-habit-duration-to-days repeat))
                              0))))))
      (unless (and due (<= today due (calendar-dayname-on-or-before 0 (+ today 6))))
        (save-excursion (outline-next-heading) (point)))))
  :config
  (require 'org-habit)
  ;; Keep event times compact instead of padding them with dots.
  ;; (setf (nth 2 org-agenda-time-grid) "")
  ;; Defer indentation until an agenda entry is displayed.
  (add-hook! org-agenda-after-show-hook
    (when (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
               org-startup-indented
               (not (bound-and-true-p org-indent-mode)))
      (org-indent-mode 1)))

  (setq org-agenda-files
        (mapcar (lambda (name)
                  (expand-file-name (concat "agenda/" name ".org") org-directory))
                '("actions" "calendar" "inbox" "personal" "routines" "someday" "work"))
        org-agenda-sticky t
        org-agenda-window-setup 'current-window
        org-agenda-restore-windows-after-quit t
        org-agenda-breadcrumbs-separator " ▸ "
        org-agenda-prefix-format '((agenda . " %? t % s")
                                   (todo . " ")
                                   (tags . " ")
                                   (search . " "))
        org-agenda-inhibit-startup t
        org-agenda-dim-blocked-tasks nil
        org-agenda-use-tag-inheritance nil
        org-agenda-ignore-properties '(stats)
        org-agenda-skip-scheduled-if-done t
        org-agenda-skip-deadline-if-done t
        org-agenda-deadline-faces '((1.001 . error)
                                    (1.0 . org-warning)
                                    (0.5 . org-upcoming-deadline)
                                    (0.0 . org-upcoming-distant-deadline))
        org-agenda-custom-commands
        '(("d" "Dashboard"
           ((agenda "")
            (alltodo "" ((org-agenda-overriding-header "Habits due this week")
                         (org-agenda-skip-function #'+agenda-skip-unless-habit-due-this-week)))
            (todo "DOING" ((org-agenda-overriding-header "Doing")))
            (todo "NEXT" ((org-agenda-overriding-header "Next")))
            (todo "TODO" ((org-agenda-overriding-header "TODO")))
            (todo "WAIT" ((org-agenda-overriding-header "Waiting")))
            (todo "SOMEDAY" ((org-agenda-overriding-header "Someday"))))
           ((org-agenda-skip-function #'+agenda-skip-habit)))
          ("g" "Doing" todo "DOING"
           ((org-agenda-skip-function #'+agenda-skip-habit)))
          ("n" "Next" todo "NEXT"
           ((org-agenda-skip-function #'+agenda-skip-habit)))
          ("w" "Waiting" todo "WAIT"
           ((org-agenda-skip-function #'+agenda-skip-habit)))
          ("p" "TODO" todo "TODO"
           ((org-agenda-skip-function #'+agenda-skip-habit)))
          ("s" "Someday" todo "SOMEDAY"
           ((org-agenda-skip-function #'+agenda-skip-habit))))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-agenda.el= section for ~calendar~

内置的 Emacs 日历（=calendar.el=），也是 Org agenda 与 diary 的老底子。

两处键位与一个 hook 之外，设置有两条：~calendar-holidays holiday-oriental-holidays~ 用中国节假日表
（配 ~calendar-chinese-all-holidays-flag t~ 把农历节日全部列出），~calendar-mark-diary-entries-flag
t~ 在日历上标出 diary 里的条目。diary 之所以能显示 Org 内容，是因为 ~diary-file~ 里有一行
~%%(org-diary)~——代码注释写明这一点，好处是保留 Org 自己的调度与重复语义，而不是把条目导出成
diary 格式。

+ 包名（Emacs 内置）：~calendar~
+ Git 仓库：
  - 源码随 Emacs 自带：<https://github.com/emacs-mirror/emacs>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
;; [calendar]
(use-package calendar
  :bind (("C-c o C" . calendar)
         :map calendar-mode-map
         ("H" . calendar-cursor-holidays))
  :hook (calendar-today-visible . calendar-mark-today)
  :config
  ;; `diary-file' contains "%%(org-diary)", which exposes Agenda entries
  ;; to Calendar while preserving Org's scheduling and repeater semantics.
  (setq calendar-chinese-all-holidays-flag t
        calendar-mark-holidays-flag t
        calendar-holidays holiday-oriental-holidays
        calendar-mark-diary-entries-flag t))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-agenda.el= section for ~calfw~

calfw 的 readme 说它是 Emacs 里的日历框架：把各种来源的日程画在 buffer 里，自带 month、1week、
2week、day 四种视图；~calfw-org~、~calfw-ical~ 是往这个框架里塞数据的两个源。

三个包都来自 =haji-ali/emacs-calfw=（同一个仓库的 fork），所以 recipe 用 ~:files~ 精确到
=calfw.el= / =calfw-org.el= / =calfw-ical.el=。~+agenda-calendar~ 把在 agenda 里选中的那一天带进
calfw（用 ~get-text-property 'day~ 取日期），内容源两个：Org 与订阅的
~chinese-days~ 节假日 ics（"中国放假"），键位用 ~calfw-org-schedule-map~。~:config~ 里四件事：
~calfw-calendar-buffer-name~ 改成 ~*Org Calendar*~（上游是 ~defconst~，~setq~ 照样能改，只是越过了
「常量」的约定）、隐藏底部的 sources footer（~:override~ 掉 ~calfw--render-footer~）、
~calfw-display-calendar-holidays t~（默认即 t，写明）、以及让 calfw buffer 也用
~+enable-conservative-scrolling~（来自 ~init-emacs-util~）。~calfw-org~ 与 ~calfw-ical~ 各自只写
~:after calfw~，没有设置。

+ 包名（GitHub haji-ali/emacs-calfw）：~calfw~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/haji-ali/emacs-calfw>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
;; [calfw] One calendar view for Org entries and subscribed days off/workdays.
(use-package calfw
  :straight (:host github :repo "haji-ali/emacs-calfw")
  :preface
  (defun +agenda-calendar (&optional view)
    "Open Org and China holidays, keeping the date selected in Agenda."
    (interactive)
    (require 'calfw-org)
    (require 'calfw-ical)
    (let ((day (when (derived-mode-p 'org-agenda-mode)
                 (get-text-property (point) 'day))))
      (calfw-open-calendar-buffer
       :date (if day (calendar-gregorian-from-absolute day)
               (calendar-current-date))
       :view view
       :custom-map calfw-org-schedule-map
       :contents-sources
       (list (calfw-org-create-source nil "Org" "SeaGreen")
             (calfw-ical-create-source "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chinese-days/dist/holidays.ics" "中国放假" "IndianRed")))))
  :config
  (setq calfw-calendar-buffer-name "*Org Calendar*"
        calfw-display-calendar-holidays t)
  (add-hook! calfw-calendar-mode-hook #'+enable-conservative-scrolling)
  (advice-add 'calfw--render-footer :override (lambda (&rest _) (string))
              '((name . hide-calendar-sources))))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package calfw-org
  :straight (:host github :repo "haji-ali/emacs-calfw"
                   :files ("calfw-org.el"))
  :after calfw)
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package calfw-ical
  :straight (:host github :repo "haji-ali/emacs-calfw"
                   :files ("calfw-ical.el"))
  :after calfw)
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-agenda.el= section for ~calfw-blocks~

README.org：给 calfw 加「时间块」视图——把带时间的日程画成色块，提供 ~block-day~、
~block-week~、~block-2-day~ … ~block-7-day~ 等视图；README 说 ~block-week~ 一律从周日起。

这个包在文件末尾直接调了 ~(calfw-blocks-mode)~，所以 ~:demand t~ 一加载就生效，:~after calfw-org~
保证加载顺序。三处改动：删掉它对 ~calfw--render-toolbar~ 的全局 override（普通 calfw 视图传两个参数，
块视图传四个，两者不能共用一个 toolbar 渲染）；用 ~+calfw-block-contents~ 过滤
~calfw--contents-put-source~ 的输出，把带时间的条目留在日期行、不带时间的挪进全天区，否则周视图会把
没时间的条目也画成块；~+calfw-event~ 把 agenda 的 text property（~time-of-day~、~duration~、
~cfw:source~）转成 calfw 的事件对象，时长默认取 ~calfw-blocks-default-event-length~。
~+agenda-calendar-blocks~ 是 ~+agenda-calendar~ 的 ~block-week~ 包装，绑在 agenda 的
~[remap org-agenda-goto-calendar]~ 上。

+ 包名（GitHub haji-ali/calfw-blocks）：~calfw-blocks~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/haji-ali/calfw-blocks>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package calfw-blocks
  :straight (:host github :repo "haji-ali/calfw-blocks")
  :after calfw-org
  :demand t
  :preface
  (defun +calfw-event (item start &optional end)
    "Return ITEM as an event, preserving Agenda properties and timing."
    (if (not (stringp item)) item
      (let* ((time (get-text-property 0 'time-of-day item))
             (minutes (when time (+ (* (/ time 100) 60) (% time 100))))
             (finish (when minutes
                       (+ minutes (round (or (get-text-property 0 'duration item)
                                             (* 60 calfw-blocks-default-event-length)))))))
        (make-calfw-event
         :title item :source (get-text-property 0 'cfw:source item)
         :start-date start
         :end-date (if finish
                       (calendar-gregorian-from-absolute
                        (+ (calendar-absolute-from-gregorian (or end start))
                           (/ finish 1440)))
                     end)
         :start-time (when minutes (list (/ minutes 60) (% minutes 60)))
         :end-time (when finish (list (% (/ finish 60) 24) (% finish 60)))))))
  (defun +calfw-block-contents (contents)
    "Convert text entries and place untimed events in the all-day area."
    (let (periods days)
      (cl-loop for (date . items) in contents
               do (if (eq date 'periods)
                      (pcase-dolist (`(,start ,end ,item) items)
                        (push (list start end (+calfw-event item start end)) periods))
                    (let ((timed
                           (cl-loop for item in items
                                    for event = (+calfw-event item date)
                                    if (calfw-event-start-time event) collect event
                                    else do (push (list date (or (calfw-event-end-date event) date)
                                                        event) periods))))
                      (when timed (push (cons date timed) days)))))
      (cons (cons 'periods (nreverse periods)) (nreverse days))))
  (defun +agenda-calendar-blocks ()
    "Open Org and subscribed holidays in a weekly time-block view."
    (interactive)
    (+agenda-calendar 'block-week))
  :config
  ;; Normal calfw views pass two arguments; the block toolbar needs four.
  ;; Block views call their own toolbar directly, so keep the normal one.
  (advice-remove 'calfw--render-toolbar #'calfw-blocks-render-toolbar)
  (advice-add 'calfw--contents-put-source :filter-return #'+calfw-block-contents))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-agenda.el= section for ~org-clock~

Org 的计时模块（源文件 Commentary：Org mode 的 clocking 代码）。这里只用它的一个入口：
~:commands (org-show-notification)~ 让 ~org-show-notification~ 需要时才加载——它是下一节 appt 的
通知出口。这个函数是 Org 自己写着「先用 handler、再 libnotify、最后退回 message」的跨平台分发
（macOS 走 ~ns-do-applescript~，Linux 走 libnotify 或 ~notify-send~），所以本配置不必为两个平台
各写一份通知代码。计时本身不是本节的事。

+ 包名（GNU ELPA）：~org-clock~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-straight/org-mode>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
;; [org-clock] Portable desktop notification backend.
(use-package org-clock
  :commands (org-show-notification))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-agenda.el= section for ~appt~

源文件 Commentary：把 diary 文件里的约会做成可见/可听的提醒（appointment）。

这一节把「弹窗」和「系统通知」并成一条路径：~appt-disp-window-function~ 换成一个调
~org-show-notification~ 的函数，于是提醒不走 appt 自己的窗口，而是变成桌面通知（格式是
「N min · 内容」）。数据来源不是 diary 而是 Org：~+appt-refresh~ 调 ~org-agenda-to-appt~ 把今天的
带时间条目灌进 appt，用 0.75 秒的 idle timer 去重（capture 与 TODO 状态变化都会触发它），另有
~after-save-hook~（只对 ~org-agenda-files~ 里的文件）与一个每天 00:01 的定时器保证跨天不漏。
五条设置都是改动：~appt-message-warning-time 15~（默认 12）、~appt-display-interval 5~（默认 3）、
~appt-display-diary nil~（默认 t，不在提醒里塞整份 diary）、~appt-audible nil~（默认 t，不响铃）、
~appt-delete-window-function #'ignore~（默认会去删窗口，桌面通知没有窗口可删）。

+ 包名（Emacs 内置）：~appt~
+ Git 仓库：
  - 源码随 Emacs 自带：<https://github.com/emacs-mirror/emacs>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
;; [appt] Convert today's timed Org entries into desktop notifications.
(use-package appt
  :require-incrementally (org-agenda t)
  :preface
  (defvar +appt-refresh-timer nil
    "Timer for deferred appointment refreshes.")
  (defun +appt-refresh ()
    "Schedule one appointment refresh after Emacs becomes idle."
    (when +appt-refresh-timer
      (cancel-timer +appt-refresh-timer))
    (setq +appt-refresh-timer
          (run-with-idle-timer 0.75 nil #'org-agenda-to-appt t)))
  :hook (((org-capture-after-finalize
           org-after-todo-state-change) . +appt-refresh))
  :config
  (setq appt-message-warning-time 15
        appt-display-interval 5
        appt-display-diary nil
        appt-audible nil
        appt-delete-window-function #'ignore
        appt-disp-window-function
        (lambda (minutes _current-time message)
          (org-show-notification
           (format "%s min · %s"
                   (string-join (ensure-list minutes) "\n")
                   (string-join (ensure-list message) "\n")))))

  (add-hook 'after-save-hook
            (lambda ()
              (when (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
                         (member buffer-file-name org-agenda-files))
                (+appt-refresh))))

  (appt-activate 1)
  (+appt-refresh)
  (run-at-time "00:01" 86400 #'+appt-refresh))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-agenda.el= section for ~org-pomodoro~

README：给 Org 加上最基本的番茄钟。默认是 25 分钟工作、5 分钟短休、20 分钟长休，每 4 个休息后
换成长休。

这里改成 30 分钟工作、15 分钟长休（短休留默认的 5），用 ~C-c o p~ 启动、对当前 entry 计时，
所以番茄钟的记录仍然落在 Org 的 clock 里，而不是另开一套日志。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~org-pomodoro~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/marcinkoziej/org-pomodoro>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
;; [org-pomodoro] Clock focused work sessions on the Org task at point.
(use-package org-pomodoro
  :straight t
  :after org
  :bind ("C-c o p" . org-pomodoro)
  :config
  (setq org-pomodoro-length 30
        org-pomodoro-long-break-length 15))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-agenda.el= section to provide the feature

把 ~init-emacs-agenda~ 记进 ~features~，模块到此结束。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-agenda.el :mkdirp yes
(provide 'init-emacs-agenda)
#+end_src

** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= module

这一章是 Git 的三层，不是一个包：内置 ~vc~ 是地基（打开文件时知道它在哪个仓库里），~diff-hl~ 在
~vc~ 之上把未提交的改动画到 fringe，Magit 家族是在同一份 Git 数据上的另一套 UI。三层共存不是浪费，
它们回答三个不同的问题。13 个包按这个顺序排：先地基与 diff，再 Magit 与其周边，最后是三个只做单个
动作的小包。

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~vc~

内置包，没有 README；据 =vc.el= 的 Commentary：它是 Emacs 的版本控制接口，文档在 Emacs 用户手册里，
支持的后端包括 CVS、RCS、SRC、SVN、Bzr、Git、Mercurial、SCCS 等。

本配置只留 Git：~vc-handled-backends '(Git)~ 之后 ~C-x v~ 系列不再去探测别的后端。
~vc-consult-headers nil~ 关闭「靠文件头里的版本标记识别工作文件」；~vc-allow-async-revert t~ 让
~C-x v @~ 的 diff 可以异步——于是本地改动还没算完也能确认 revert；
~vc-allow-rewriting-published-history t~ 是唯一一处值得盯着的宽松值：它连询问都不给，直接允许改写
已发布历史（defcustom 自己的建议是 ~ask~ 或 ~nil~）。~vc-dir-auto-hide-up-to-date 'revert~ 选的是
中间那档：已同步/已忽略的文件只在刷新（~g~）时才从 ~vc-dir~ 列表里消失，不自动消失。

+ 包名（Emacs 内置）：~vc~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-mirror/emacs>（源码随 Emacs 自带）

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;;; -*- lexical-binding: t -*-

;; [vc-mode] Version control interface
(use-package vc
  :config
  (setq vc-handled-backends '(Git)
        vc-consult-headers nil
        vc-allow-async-revert t
        vc-allow-rewriting-published-history t
        vc-dir-auto-hide-up-to-date 'revert))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~git-link~

README 第一句：生成 GitHub/Bitbucket/GitLab 等仓库里当前文件与当前行（或活动区域）的 URL 的交互命令；
~git-link-commit~ 给当前 commit、~git-link-homepage~ 给仓库首页，URL 进 kill ring。

~C-, g~ 前缀下正好对应这三件事：~l~ = 当前文件当前位置、~c~ = 当前 commit、~h~ = 首页。
它和本模块后面的 ~browse-at-remote~ 功能重叠，两个都留着是因为 git-link 的三个命令在 Dired、Magit、
VC revision 与 Tramp 里都能用（README 明说），而 browse-at-remote 的入口是 ~C-x v~ 前缀下的一个键。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~git-link~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/sshaw/git-link>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; [git-link] Get remote repo URL for buffer location
(use-package git-link
  :straight t
  :bind (("C-, g l" . git-link)
         ("C-, g c" . git-link-commit)
         ("C-, g h" . git-link-homepage)))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~diff-hl~

README 开头：~diff-hl-mode~ 在窗口侧边高亮未提交的改动，可以逐个跳转、选择性 revert，Git 下还能
stage/unstage——也就是通常说的「source control gutter indicators」；非图形终端没有 fringe 时，只要
~diff-hl-fallback-to-margin~ 非 nil 就退回用 margin。

三个 hook 分别覆盖全局、~vc-dir~ 与 dired；六个键在 ~C-c v~ 下（显示 hunk、revert、前后跳、stage）。
~vc-git-diff-switches '("--histogram")~ 换 diff 算法；~diff-hl-fallback-to-margin t~ 是上面那条终端
路径。两处 fringe face 被换成 ~diff-refine-*~ 系列，好处是颜色跟着主题的 diff 面走，不必单独调。

它画的是 ~vc~ 的状态，所以「什么时候重算」要自己接四根线：advice ~vc-refresh-state~（状态刷新之后）、
advice ~ws-butler-after-save~（保存后 ~ws-butler~ 会把 buffer 恢复成「没改过」，diff 必须跟着更新）、
~after-focus-change-function~（切窗口后不同 buffer 的 diff 各自重算）、以及 ~magit-post-refresh-hook~
（放在 ~with-eval-after-load 'magit~ 里，没装 Magit 就不挂）。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~diff-hl~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/dgutov/diff-hl>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; [diff-hl] Highlight uncommitted changes using VC
(use-package diff-hl
  :straight t
  :hook ((after-init . global-diff-hl-mode)
         (vc-dir-mode  . diff-hl-dir-mode)
         (dired-mode   . diff-hl-dired-mode))
  :bind (:map diff-hl-mode-map
              ("C-c v v" . diff-hl-show-hunk)
              ("C-c v r" . diff-hl-revert-hunk)
              ("C-c v [" . diff-hl-previous-hunk)
              ("C-c v ]" . diff-hl-next-hunk)
              ("C-c v s" . diff-hl-stage-current-hunk)
              ("C-c v u" . diff-hl-revert-hunk))
  :config
  (setq
   ;; A slightly faster algorithm for diffing
   vc-git-diff-switches '("--histogram")
   ;; Use margins in terminal frames where fringes don't exist.
   diff-hl-fallback-to-margin t)

  (defun +diff-hl--vc-face (type _pos)
    (pcase type
      ('insert 'diff-refine-added)
      ('delete 'diff-refine-removed)
      ('change 'diff-refine-changed)))
  (setq diff-hl-fringe-face-function #'+diff-hl--vc-face
        diff-hl-fringe-reference-face-function #'+diff-hl--vc-face)

  ;; Integration with magit
  (with-eval-after-load 'magit
    (add-hook! magit-post-refresh-hook #'diff-hl-magit-post-refresh))

  ;; WORKAROUND: Integration with ws-butler
  (with-eval-after-load 'ws-butler
    (advice-add #'ws-butler-after-save :after #'diff-hl-update))

  ;; HACK: Update after vc-state refreshed
  (advice-add #'vc-refresh-state :after #'diff-hl-update)

  ;; Update after focus change for different mode.
  (defun +diff-hl-update-after-focus-change ()
    (cond ((bound-and-true-p diff-hl-mode)
           (diff-hl-update))
          ((bound-and-true-p diff-hl-dir-mode)
           (diff-hl-dir-update))
          ((bound-and-true-p diff-hl-dired-mode)
           (diff-hl-dired-update))))
  (add-function :after after-focus-change-function
                #'+diff-hl-update-after-focus-change))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~magit~

README：Magit 是用 Emacs Lisp 写的 Git 界面，目标是完整的 Git porcelain——README 自己的说法是
「只有 Magit 和 Git 本身配得上 porcelain 这个词」。

~:require-incrementally~（~init-emacs-straight~ 里给 once 的 ~once-require-incrementally~ 起的别名）后面
是一串子库，末尾的 ~t~ 会被 once 替换成包自己的主库，于是连 =magit.el= 本体也进惰性队列：启动后由
空闲计时器逐个 require，而不是一次性把十几个库全读进来。

~:config~ 里五个变量都是「关掉」：不画空白差异、不替用户保存仓库里所有 buffer（太魔幻）、commit
buffer 不显示相关 ref（很少有用，只是拖慢）、diff 用 indicator faces、不标注行尾空白。
~+magit-kill-buffers~ 换掉 ~magit-bury-buffer-function~：恢复窗口布局，并且在当前 buffer 确实是
~magit-status-mode~ 时才遍历 magit buffer 去杀，其中还有进程在跑的退一步用 bury。
最后一个函数是给下一个包用的开关：~+toggle-magit-difftastic~ 通过 ~transient-append-suffix~ 往
magit 的 diff 菜单尾部加一个 "D"，而不是另起一个入口。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~magit~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/magit/magit>
+ 官方手册：<https://docs.magit.vc/magit>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; [magit] Version control interface
(use-package magit
  :straight t
  :require-incrementally
  (compat with-editor eieio transient
          magit-section magit-base magit-git magit-mode magit-process
          git-commit magit-transient magit-margin magit-autorevert magit-core
          magit-diff magit-log magit-wip magit-apply magit-repos t)
  :bind (("C-x g" . magit))
  :hook ((magit-process-mode . goto-address-mode))
  :config
  (setq
   ;; dont paint whitespace
   magit-diff-paint-whitespace nil
   ;; Don't autosave repo buffers. This is too magical
   magit-save-repository-buffers nil
   ;; Don't display parent/related refs in commit buffers; they are rarely helpful and only add to runtime costs.
   magit-revision-insert-related-refs nil
   magit-diff-use-indicator-faces t
   magit-diff-highlight-trailing nil)

  ;; Exterminate Magit buffers
  (defun +magit-kill-buffers (&rest _)
    "Restore window configuration and kill all Magit buffers."
    (interactive)
    (magit-restore-window-configuration)
    (let ((buffers (magit-mode-get-buffers)))
      (when (eq major-mode 'magit-status-mode)
        (mapc (lambda (buf)
                (with-current-buffer buf
                  (if (and magit-this-process
                           (eq (process-status magit-this-process) 'run))
                      (bury-buffer buf)
                    (kill-buffer buf))))
              buffers))))
  (setq magit-bury-buffer-function #'+magit-kill-buffers)

  (defun +toggle-magit-difftastic ()
    "Toggle `magit-difftastic-mode' in Magit buffers."
    (interactive)
    (magit-difftastic-mode
     (if magit-difftastic-mode -1 1)))
  (transient-append-suffix 'magit-diff '(-1 -1)
    [("D" "Difftastic Diff" +toggle-magit-difftastic)]))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~magit-difftastic~

README：在 Magit 自己的 buffer（status、diff、revision）里用 difftastic 渲染 diff，输出是真正的
Magit section——每个改动文件一个 file section，正文按可折叠的 chunk 分段，staging 仍然可用，渲染不了
的地方退回原版 Magit。

recipe 指向 rschmukler 的仓库而不是 MELPA，所以装的是这个 fork。三个 setq 都是显示层：内联渲染、
不要行号、不要语法高亮（diff 里两种颜色已经够用，再加上语法着色反而更难扫）。

~:after magit~ 让它等 Magit 加载后再进惰性队列；真正的加载时机是上一节的 "D" 开关第一次被按。

+ 包名（GitHub rschmukler/magit-difftastic）：~magit-difftastic~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/rschmukler/magit-difftastic>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package magit-difftastic
  :straight (:host github :repo "rschmukler/magit-difftastic")
  :require-incrementally t
  :after magit
  :config
  (setq magit-difftastic-display "inline"
        magit-difftastic-line-numbers nil
        magit-difftastic-syntax-highlight nil))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~ghub~

README：Ghub 给「从 Emacs 调各种 forge 的 API」提供基础函数（~ghub-get~、~ghub-request~ 一类），
支持 GitHub 的 REST 与 GraphQL，以及 GitLab、Gitea、Gogs、Bitbucket 的 REST；访问 GitHub 时它还负责
创建与存储 token。

这里不要它的 token 管理：~+ghub--token-from-gh-cli~ 直接跑 ~gh auth token --hostname github.com~，
结果只写进内存变量 ~+ghub-gh-token-cache~，配置里不出现 token、也不落盘。
把这条路径接进上游的是 ~+ghub-token-from-gh-cli-a~：一条 ~:around~ ~ghub--token~ 的 advice，只在
forge 为 ~nil~/~github~ 时改道，其它 forge 交给原函数——因为这条路径只解决 GitHub 的凭据。
本模块的 ~forge~ 与 ~remoto~ 都走它。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~ghub~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/magit/ghub>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; Use the GitHub CLI credential directly.  Keep the token in memory only
;; and fetch it lazily.
(use-package ghub
  :straight t
  :preface
  (defvar +ghub-gh-token-cache nil)

  (defun +ghub--token-from-gh-cli ()
    "Return the GitHub CLI token, caching it for this Emacs session."
    (or +ghub-gh-token-cache
        (let ((gh (or (executable-find "gh")
                      (user-error "GitHub CLI executable not found"))))
          (with-temp-buffer
            (call-process gh nil t nil "auth" "token" "--hostname" "github.com")
            (setq +ghub-gh-token-cache (string-trim (buffer-string)))))))
  :config
  (defadvice! +ghub-token-from-gh-cli-a (fn host username package
                                            &optional nocreate forge)
    :around #'ghub--token
    "Use `gh auth token' directly for GitHub; otherwise call FN."
    (if (memq forge '(nil github))
        (+ghub--token-from-gh-cli)
      (funcall fn host username package nocreate forge))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~forge~

README：在 Magit 里操作 Git forge 上的 issue、PR 与 topic（README 只有一句「Work with Git forges,
such as Github and Gitlab, from the comfort of Magit」，细节都在手册里）。

它依赖 ghub 取 token，所以上一节那条 advice 就是它能用 GitHub 的原因。~:init~ 里把
~forge-post-fallback-directory~ 指到 no-littering 的 =var/= 下并立刻 ~make-directory~——草稿目录要
在第一次用它之前就存在；这个变量是「远端没有 draft 功能时的落点」。一个 ~custom-face~ 让 topic
label 用 variable-pitch 且小一号，是纯显示层。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~forge~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/magit/forge>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package forge
  :straight t
  :require-incrementally t
  :after magit
  :init
  (setq forge-post-fallback-directory
        (no-littering-expand-var-file-name "forge/drafts/"))
  (make-directory forge-post-fallback-directory t)
  :custom-face
  (forge-topic-label ((t (:inherit variable-pitch :height 0.9 :width condensed :weight regular :underline unspecified)))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~magit-todos~

README：把源码注释与 Org 文件里的关键字条目汇总到 Magit status buffer 里，激活一项就跳到它在文件里
的位置；默认用 ~hl-todo~ 的关键字表，去掉 ~NOTE~ 之类的几个。

~magit-todos-update nil~ 关掉自动扫描：默认值 ~t~ 会在每次 magit status 刷新时扫仓库，这里改成只在
手动调用 ~magit-todos-update~ 时扫，启动后的第一个 status 就不会因为扫全仓库而卡住。
~(magit-todos-mode 1)~ 放在 ~:init~ 而不是 ~:config~：它是 autoload 的 global minor mode，而
~:config~ 只在包加载之后才跑，那时第一个 status buffer 可能已经画完了（这块代码本身靠
~:require-incrementally~ 慢慢补齐）。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~magit-todos~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/alphapapa/magit-todos>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; Show TODOs in magit
(use-package magit-todos
  :straight t
  :require-incrementally t
  :after magit
  :init
  (setq magit-todos-update nil)
  (magit-todos-mode 1))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~remoto~

README：注册一个基于 ~file-name-handler-alist~ 的虚拟文件系统，把标准文件操作翻译成远端 API 调用，
于是不用 clone 就能用 ~find-file~、dired、补全去浏览任意 GitHub 或 Codeberg 仓库。

两处 advice 都在改它的认证时机：~remoto--warm-auth~ 被 ~:override~ 成 ~ignore~（不预热，登录流程
留给真正要用的那一刻）；~remoto--find-token~ 被 ~:around~ 到 ~+remoto-github-token-from-gh-cli~，
同样从 gh CLI 取 token，但判断的是 forge 名字而不是类型谓词——~remoto-forgejo~ 继承
~remoto-github~，类型谓词对 Codeberg 也为真，用类型判断会把 GitHub 的 token 递给 Codeberg。
注释里记了版本事实：remoto 2.5 删了 ~remoto--find-github-token~，token 现在由 ~remoto--find-token~ 读。

+ 包名（GitHub agzam/remoto.el）：~remoto~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/agzam/remoto.el>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; [remoto] Browse GitHub repositories without cloning
(use-package remoto
  :straight (:host github :repo "agzam/remoto.el")
  :config
  ;; remoto 2.5 removed `remoto--find-github-token'.  GitHub tokens are now
  ;; read by `remoto--find-token'; keep taking them from `gh auth token'.
  (defun +remoto-github-token-from-gh-cli (fn forge)
    "Return the GitHub CLI token for a GitHub FORGE; otherwise call FN."
    ;; `remoto-forgejo' includes `remoto-github', so the type predicate is
    ;; true for Codeberg too.  Match the GitHub forge by name.
    (if (eq (remoto-forge-name forge) 'github)
        (+ghub--token-from-gh-cli)
      (funcall fn forge)))
  (advice-add #'remoto--warm-auth :override #'ignore)
  (advice-add #'remoto--find-token :around #'+remoto-github-token-from-gh-cli))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~smerge-mode~

内置包，没有 README；据 =smerge-mode.el= 的 Commentary：它是 emerge/ediff 的轻量替代，专门处理
文件里留下的 ~<<<<<<<~ 冲突标记，Commentary 里给出的自动开启写法就是本节这两个函数的原型。

~+smerge-try-smerge~ 挂在 ~find-file~ 上：只在「有 ~^<<<<<<< ~ 行」且「文件受 VC 管」时才 require
并开 ~smerge-mode~，所以普通文件不会被扫一遍。另一半是让它进诊断面板：~+smerge-flymake-backend~ 是
一个 flymake backend，把每处未解决的冲突报成一条 ~flymake-error~；~+smerge-flymake-setup-h~ 在
~smerge-mode~ 打开时把这个 backend 加进 buffer-local 的 ~flymake-diagnostic-functions~ 并启动
flymake。于是冲突从「看不见的文本」变成 flymake 列表里的一项。

+ 包名（Emacs 内置）：~smerge-mode~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-mirror/emacs>（源码随 Emacs 自带）

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; [smerge] Highlight all the conflicted regions for git
(use-package smerge-mode
  :preface
  (defun +smerge-flymake-backend (report-fn &rest _args)
    "Report unresolved merge conflicts in the current buffer."
    (let (diagnostics)
      (save-excursion
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (while (smerge-find-conflict)
          (let ((beg (match-beginning 0)))
            (push (flymake-make-diagnostic
                   (current-buffer) beg
                   (save-excursion (goto-char beg) (line-end-position))
                   'flymake-error "Unresolved merge conflict")
                  diagnostics))))
      (funcall report-fn (nreverse diagnostics))))

  (defun +smerge-flymake-setup-h ()
    "Add the Smerge Flymake backend to a conflicted buffer."
    (when smerge-mode
      (add-hook 'flymake-diagnostic-functions
                #'+smerge-flymake-backend nil t)
      (if (bound-and-true-p flymake-mode)
          (flymake-start)
        (flymake-mode 1))))

  (defun +smerge-try-smerge ()
    (when (and buffer-file-name
               (save-excursion
                 (goto-char (point-min))
                 (re-search-forward "^<<<<<<< " nil t))
               (vc-backend buffer-file-name))
      (require 'smerge-mode)
      (smerge-mode 1)))
  :hook ((find-file . +smerge-try-smerge)
         (smerge-mode . +smerge-flymake-setup-h)))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~browse-at-remote~

readme.rst：打开 github/gitlab/bitbucket/stash/git.savannah.gnu.org/sourcehut/gitea 上对应的链接，
支持 file buffer、dired、代表代码的 magit buffer 与 ~vc-annotate~。

本配置只做一件事：把命令绑到 ~vc-prefix-map~ 的 ~B~，于是它在 ~C-x v B~ 上——放在 VC 前缀里而不是
全局键，是因为它跟 ~C-x v~ 的其它命令一样是「对当前文件的版本控制动作」。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~browse-at-remote~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/rmuslimov/browse-at-remote>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; [browse-at-remote] Open github/gitlab/bitbucket page
(use-package browse-at-remote
  :straight t
  :bind (:map vc-prefix-map
              ("B" . browse-at-remote)))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~git-modes~

README：为各种 Git 配置文件提供 major mode——三个库各自 autoload 到对应文件名：
~gitattributes-mode~、~gitconfig-mode~（含 ~.gitmodules~）、~gitignore-mode~，后两个从
~conf-unix-mode~ 派生。

本配置只写 ~:straight t~，没有任何设置，也没有 ~:config~：它靠 autoload 生效，打开
~.gitconfig~／~.gitignore~ 时 Emacs 自己会按文件名找到 mode，无需提前加载。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~git-modes~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/magit/git-modes>
+ 官方手册：<https://elpa.nongnu.org/nongnu/git-modes.html>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; [git-modes] Git configuration major modes
(use-package git-modes
  :straight t)
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section for ~abridge-diff~

README：把 refine 过的 diff hunk「截短」（abridge）——长行文件（LaTeX、整段一行的文本）里，逐行
diff 即使开了 hunk refining 也很难读，这个包只显示改动的词和少量上下文。

~:after magit~ 加上 ~magit-diff-visit-file~ hook，于是它只在「从 magit 打开一个文件的 diff」时生效，
是局部 minor mode，不全局开；关掉它用 ~C-- M-x abridge-diff-mode~（README 给的退出口）。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~abridge-diff~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/jdtsmith/abridge-diff>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
;; [abridge-diff]
(use-package abridge-diff
  :straight t
  :after magit
  :hook (magit-diff-visit-file . abridge-diff-mode))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-vcs.el= section to provide the feature

~provide~ 把 ~init-emacs-vcs~ 登记成 feature，这个模块的加载到此结束。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-vcs.el :mkdirp yes
(provide 'init-emacs-vcs)
#+end_src

** The =init-emacs-tools.el= module

这一章只有两个包，共同点是「只读」：~speedbar~ 是目录/结构树，~emureader~ 是 PDF/EPUB 阅读器。
它们都不参与编辑流程，所以这一节短得只有两个 ~use-package~ 和一个 ~provide~。

*** The =init-emacs-tools.el= section for ~speedbar~

内置包，没有 README；据 =speedbar.el= 的 Commentary：它提供一个 frame 或 window，在里面显示文件
与文件内的位置，可以点开；进一步的自定义见 speedbar 手册。

两行设置：~speedbar-prefer-window t~ 让它占用自己的 window 而不是另开一帧（这样它进的是本配置的
窗口布局，而不是一个脱离 ~winner~/~popper~ 管辖的新 frame），宽度固定 30。
它跟 ~init-emacs-window~ 的耦合在那一节：zoom 把 ~speedbar-mode~ 列进 ~zoom-ignored-major-modes~ 并给
这个 window 设了 ~window-size-fixed 'width~，两边配合，一开 speedbar 才不会被自动平衡改宽度。

+ 包名（Emacs 内置）：~speedbar~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-mirror/emacs>（源码随 Emacs 自带）

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-tools.el :mkdirp yes
;;; -*- lexical-binding: t -*-

;; [speedbar]
(use-package speedbar
  :init
  (setq speedbar-prefer-window t
        speedbar-window-default-width 30))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-tools.el= section for ~emureader~

README.org：面向 GNU Emacs 的全能文档阅读器，支持多种主要文档格式，自称要做 ~doc-view~、=nov.el=、
~pdf-tools~ 的替代（drop-in replacement）；原名 The Emacs Reader，包名与动态模块名都改过
（~reader~ → ~emureader~，~render-core~ → ~emureader-dyn~）。

它是 C 扩展包，所以 recipe 比别的包长，而且 recipe 本身就是这个包在本机的安装说明：
~:files~ 用 ~module-file-suffix~ 拼出构建产物名，于是 macOS（~.dylib~）与 Linux（~.so~）都命中；
~:pre-build~ 直接跑 ~make~，并在命令行上递三个赋值：~CC=cc~、~USE_PKGCONFIG=yes~ 与
~PKG_CONFIG_PATH=...~。后两个的原因写在注释里——它的 Makefile 一发现 nix 就强制
~USE_PKGCONFIG=off~，然后转头去找 Homebrew 的 mupdf；命令行赋值优先级更高，所以这里直接把
~USE_PKGCONFIG=yes~ 与 Home Manager 的 pkgconfig 路径递进去。删掉这段 recipe，包还在，但下次重编译
就找不到 mupdf。

+ 包名（Codeberg MonadicSheep/emacs-reader）：~emureader~
+ Git 仓库：
  - Codeberg：<https://codeberg.org/MonadicSheep/emacs-reader>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-tools.el :mkdirp yes
;; [emureader] read docs in emacs.  The package renamed from `reader' and
;; its module from `render-core' to `emureader-dyn' (see its Makefile).
(use-package emureader
  :straight  `(emureader :type git :host codeberg :repo "MonadicSheep/emacs-reader"
                         :package "emureader" :local-repo "emacs-reader"
                         :files (:defaults ,(concat "emureader-dyn" module-file-suffix))
                         ;; The Makefile forces USE_PKGCONFIG off when it finds nix, then
                         ;; looks for a Homebrew mupdf.  A command-line assignment wins
                         ;; over that, and this machine's mupdf comes from Home Manager.
                         :pre-build ("make" "all" "CC=cc" "USE_PKGCONFIG=yes"
                                     ,(concat "PKG_CONFIG_PATH="
                                              (expand-file-name "~/.local/state/home-manager/gcroots/current-home/home-path/lib/pkgconfig")))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-tools.el= section to provide the feature

~provide~ 把 ~init-emacs-tools~ 登记成 feature，这个模块的加载到此结束。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-tools.el :mkdirp yes
(provide 'init-emacs-tools)
#+end_src

** The =init-emacs-mail.el= module

这一章是一封本地邮件客户端的全部：~mu~ 做索引与检索，~mu4e~ 做 UI，~msmtp~ 发信。它与别的模块有
三点不同——包不是 straight 装的（来自 Nix 的 ~mu4e~ output），凭据不在本文件里（地址在 gitignored
的 =private.el=，密码在 Keychain），整段在找不到 ~mu4e~ 时静默跳过。所以这一章的顺序是「先数据，再说
设置，最后是加载开关」。

*** The =init-emacs-mail.el= section for credentials and addresses

~mu4e~ 是 ~mu~ 的 Emacs 前端（mu 负责索引与检索，mu4e 负责 buffer），它没有可以在这里引用的
README：整个包来自 Nix 的 ~mu4e~ output。版本必须与 ~mu~ 二进制一致，所以不能让 straight 去克隆
djcb/mu——这就是本模块不出现 ~:straight~ 的原因。

三个变量先声明成 ~nil~，再由 =private.el= 覆盖：那个文件 gitignored，用一次 ~load~（~nomessage~）
读进来。它们的默认值故意是 ~nil~ 而不是占位字符串，于是「没配」在代码里可判——后面 ~+mu4e-apply~
每造一个 context 都套了一层 ~+mu4e-address-p~，地址为空就干脆不造。本文件里没有任何密码。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-mail.el :mkdirp yes
;;; -*- lexical-binding: t -*-

;; Local mail reader. Upstream stopped at Himalaya mode hooks and does not
;; load a mail client. mu and mu4e come from the same Nix package (the
;; `mu4e' output); do not clone djcb/mu with straight or the binary and
;; Elisp versions diverge.
;;
;; Addresses stay in gitignored private.el:
;;   (setq +mu4e-gmail-address "you@gmail.com"
;;         +mu4e-qq-address "you@qq.com"
;;         +mu4e-full-name "Your Name")
;; Sync and SMTP passwords stay in the Keychain, not here.

(defvar +mu4e-gmail-address nil
  "Gmail address for the mu4e gmail context. Set in private.el.")

(defvar +mu4e-qq-address nil
  "QQ address for the mu4e qq context. Set in private.el.")

(defvar +mu4e-full-name nil
  "Display name used by mu4e contexts. Set in private.el.")

(let ((file (expand-file-name "private.el" user-emacs-directory)))
  (when (file-exists-p file)
    (load file nil 'nomessage)))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-mail.el= section for ~+mu4e-apply~

~+mu4e-apply~ 是「一遍把所有设置做掉」的地方。它被收成一个可重复调用的函数，是因为 =private.el=
改过之后重调它就行，不需要重启 Emacs；函数体里 ~require~ 了 ~message~ 与 ~smtpmail~，因为它要设
发信相关的变量。

四个 helper 各有分工：~+mu4e-address-p~ 判「非空字符串」；~+mu4e-program~ 找不到可执行文件时退回
名字本身，于是 ~setopt~ 不会因为路径是 ~nil~ 而报错；~+set-msmtp-account~ 挂在
~message-send-mail-hook~ 上，按 From 头挑 msmtp 账户（~-a gmail~ / ~-a qq~），保证「这封信从哪个
地址发」和「用哪个账号发」永远一致；~+mu4e-make-context~ 造一个 ~mu4e-context~，~match-func~ 按
收件人匹配。再一个是显示层的 ~+mu4e-split-view-sensibly~，按 ~split-width-threshold~ 在左右/上下之间
选（竖向时标题在左）。

设置本身有两处值得单独看。文件夹名必须写死成 IMAP 上的真名（Gmail 的 =[Gmail]/Sent Mail= 即使 web
UI 是中文也不变），因为 mu4e 是按路径同步的；~mu4e-context-policy 'pick-first~ 决定读信时优先命中
哪个 context，~mu4e-compose-context-policy 'ask~ 决定写信时要不要问。另外整段 headers 的 thread 前缀
与各类 mark 被换成 unicode/emoji，~*mu4e-update*~ 被钉在下方 10% 高度的窗口里，不抢正文。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-mail.el :mkdirp yes
(defun +mu4e-address-p (address)
  "Return non-nil when ADDRESS is a non-empty string."
  (and (stringp address) (not (string-empty-p address))))

(defun +mu4e-program (name)
  "Return the absolute path of NAME, or NAME when it is not installed yet."
  (or (executable-find name) name))

(defun +set-msmtp-account ()
  "Select the msmtp account from the From header."
  (when (message-mail-p)
    (let* ((from (save-restriction
                   (message-narrow-to-headers)
                   (message-fetch-field "from")))
           (account (cond
                     ((and (+mu4e-address-p +mu4e-gmail-address)
                           from
                           (string-match-p (regexp-quote +mu4e-gmail-address) from))
                      "gmail")
                     ((and (+mu4e-address-p +mu4e-qq-address)
                           from
                           (string-match-p (regexp-quote +mu4e-qq-address) from))
                      "qq"))))
      (when account
        (setq message-sendmail-extra-arguments (list "-a" account))))))

(defun +mu4e-make-context (name address vars)
  "Build a mu4e context named NAME for ADDRESS with extra VARS."
  (make-mu4e-context
   :name name
   :enter-func (lambda () (mu4e-message "Enter %s context" name))
   :leave-func (lambda () (mu4e-message "Leave %s context" name))
   :match-func (lambda (msg)
                 (when msg
                   (mu4e-message-contact-field-matches msg :to address)))
   :vars (append `((user-mail-address . ,address)
                   (user-full-name . ,(or +mu4e-full-name "")))
                 vars)))

(defun +mu4e-split-view-sensibly (&rest _)
  "Use a side-by-side view only when the window is wide enough.

`vertical' puts the headers on the left. `horizontal' keeps them on top.
The cutoff is `split-width-threshold', the same value roife leaves at the
Emacs default."
  (setq mu4e-split-view
        (if (>= (window-body-width) split-width-threshold)
            'vertical
          'horizontal)))

(defun +mu4e-apply ()
  "Apply the local mu4e settings. Safe to call again after private.el changes."
  (require 'message)
  (require 'smtpmail)
  (setopt mu4e-mu-binary (+mu4e-program "mu")
          mu4e-get-mail-command (format "%s -a" (+mu4e-program "mbsync"))
          mu4e-update-interval 300
          mu4e-attachment-dir "~/Desktop"
          mu4e-change-filenames-when-moving t
          message-kill-buffer-on-exit t
          send-mail-function #'message-send-mail-with-sendmail
          message-send-mail-function #'message-send-mail-with-sendmail
          sendmail-program (+mu4e-program "msmtp")
          message-sendmail-envelope-from 'header
          mu4e-use-fancy-chars t
          mu4e-context-policy 'pick-first
          mu4e-compose-context-policy 'ask
          mu4e-maildir-shortcuts
          '((:maildir "/gmail/INBOX" :key ?g)
            (:maildir "/qq/INBOX" :key ?q)))
  (setq mu4e-headers-thread-single-orphan-prefix '("─>" . "─▶")
        mu4e-headers-thread-orphan-prefix '("┬>" . "┬▶ ")
        mu4e-headers-thread-connection-prefix '("│ " . "│ ")
        mu4e-headers-thread-first-child-prefix '("├>" . "├▶")
        mu4e-headers-thread-child-prefix '("├>" . "├▶")
        mu4e-headers-thread-last-child-prefix '("└>" . "╰▶")
        mu4e-headers-unread-mark '("u" . "📩 ")
        mu4e-headers-draft-mark '("D" . "🚧 ")
        mu4e-headers-flagged-mark '("F" . "🚩 ")
        mu4e-headers-new-mark '("N" . "✨ ")
        mu4e-headers-passed-mark '("P" . "↪ ")
        mu4e-headers-replied-mark '("R" . "↩ ")
        mu4e-headers-seen-mark '("S" . " ")
        mu4e-headers-trashed-mark '("T" . "🗑️ ")
        mu4e-headers-attach-mark '("a" . "📎 ")
        mu4e-headers-encrypted-mark '("x" . "🔑 ")
        mu4e-headers-signed-mark '("s" . "🖊 ")
        mu4e-headers-list-mark '("l" . "🔈 ")
        mu4e-headers-personal-mark '("p" . "👨 ")
        mu4e-headers-calendar-mark '("c" . "📅 ")
        mu4e-contexts
        (delq nil
              (list
               (when (+mu4e-address-p +mu4e-gmail-address)
                 (+mu4e-make-context
                  "gmail" +mu4e-gmail-address
                  ;; Gmail IMAP keeps these names even when the web UI is Chinese.
                  '((mu4e-drafts-folder . "/gmail/[Gmail]/Drafts")
                    (mu4e-refile-folder . "/gmail/[Gmail]/All Mail")
                    (mu4e-sent-folder . "/gmail/[Gmail]/Sent Mail")
                    (mu4e-trash-folder . "/gmail/[Gmail]/Trash"))))
               (when (+mu4e-address-p +mu4e-qq-address)
                 (+mu4e-make-context
                  "qq" +mu4e-qq-address
                  '((mu4e-drafts-folder . "/qq/Drafts")
                    (mu4e-refile-folder . "/qq/Archive")
                    (mu4e-sent-folder . "/qq/Sent Messages")
                    (mu4e-trash-folder . "/qq/Deleted Messages")))))))
  (add-hook 'message-send-mail-hook #'+set-msmtp-account)
  (advice-add #'mu4e-display-buffer :before #'+mu4e-split-view-sensibly)
  (add-to-list 'display-buffer-alist
               '("\\*mu4e-update\\*"
                 (display-buffer-below-selected)
                 (window-height . 0.1))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-mail.el= section for ~+mu4e-lisp-dir~

这两块解决的是同一件事：=mu4e.el= 在哪。它沿着 ~executable-find "mu"~ 的符号链接往上爬，找
=share/emacs/site-lisp/mu4e=——nix-darwin 的用户 profile 只 link 了 =bin/mu= 而没 link
=share/emacs=，但它指向的 home-manager 目标里两者都有，所以要顺着 symlink 走一层。
~+mu4e--prefix~ 只是从 =.../bin/mu= 取两级目录。

找不到时它返回 ~nil~，~when-let~ 于是不 ~add-to-list~，启动照常往下走——这是本章「找不到就静默跳过」
那半句的实现。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-mail.el :mkdirp yes
;; `use-package' is an autoload in Emacs 31, so `fboundp' is true even
;; under emacs -Q. Batch tests apply the settings at load time. A normal
;; session defers them until `mu4e' is invoked. If the Nix output is not
;; on `load-path' yet, do nothing so startup still succeeds.
(defun +mu4e--prefix (path)
  "Return the installation prefix that contains PATH's bin directory."
  (file-name-directory (directory-file-name (file-name-directory path))))

(defun +mu4e-lisp-dir ()
  "Find mu4e.el next to the Nix profile link for `mu', not the bare store bin.

nix-darwin's user profile links `bin/mu' but not `share/emacs'. The
home-manager path that the symlink points at still has both."
  (let ((path (executable-find "mu"))
        (dir nil))
    (while (and path (not dir))
      (let ((candidate (expand-file-name "share/emacs/site-lisp/mu4e"
                                         (+mu4e--prefix path))))
        (if (file-exists-p (expand-file-name "mu4e.el" candidate))
            (setq dir candidate)
          (let ((link (file-symlink-p path)))
            (setq path (and link
                            (expand-file-name link (file-name-directory path))))))))
    dir))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-mail.el= section for the ~mu4e~ load switch

~use-package~ 在 Emacs 31 是 autoload，所以 ~fboundp~ 恒真，「mu4e 已装」这个判断不能用它——这是
这里不写 ~(when (fboundp 'mu4e) ...)~ 的原因。

~cond~ 三分支：~locate-library~ 找不到就什么都不做；~noninteractive~（batch）直接 ~require~ 并应用
设置，便于测试；正常会话用 ~use-package~ 的 ~:commands (mu4e)~ 延到第一次调用 ~mu4e~ 时才 ~require~，
~:config~ 里再调一次 ~+mu4e-apply~。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-mail.el :mkdirp yes
(when-let ((dir (+mu4e-lisp-dir)))
  (add-to-list 'load-path dir))

(cond
 ((not (locate-library "mu4e"))
  nil)
 (noninteractive
  (require 'mu4e)
  (+mu4e-apply))
 (t
  (use-package mu4e
    :commands (mu4e)
    :config
    (+mu4e-apply))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-mail.el= section to provide the feature

~provide~ 把 ~init-emacs-mail~ 登记成 feature，这个模块的加载到此结束。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-mail.el :mkdirp yes
(provide 'init-emacs-mail)
#+end_src

** The =init-emacs-web.el= module

这是全文第二大的模块，但里面是四个不相干的入口：~elfeed~（RSS）、~telega~（Telegram）、~chirp~
（X/Twitter）、以及三个浏览器入口（~browse-url~、~eww~、~xwidget~）。规模几乎全在 ~elfeed~ 与
~telega~ 上：前者的辅助函数比 ~use-package~ 本体还长，后者几乎全是针对上游行为的 hack。四块之间没有
共享代码，只有「都是网络入口」这一个共同点。

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for the local ~elfeed~ atom

~elfeed~ README：可扩展的 web feed 阅读器，支持 Atom、RSS 与 JSON Feed，UI 受 notmuch 启发，
数据库格式稳定且不打算改。

本配置的 feed 有两类来源：远端 URL，以及 =var/rss/= 下的本地 atom（由 =scripts/hn-elfeed.py=、
=scripts/reddit-elfeed.py= 生成，用 =file://= 注册）。本地 atom 意味着「feed 列表里的一项可能还不
存在」，所以先有这一组构造函数：~+elfeed-local-dir~ 走 no-littering 的 =var/rss/=，
~+elfeed-hn-atom~ 与 ~+elfeed-reddit-atom~ 定死文件名，~+elfeed-file-feed~/~+elfeed-hn-feed~ 拼出
=file://= 条目，~+elfeed-feed-url~ 统一取 URL——因为列表里既有裸字符串也有 ~(url . tags)~ 对。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
;;; -*- lexical-binding: t -*-

(defvar +elfeed-local-dir (no-littering-expand-var-file-name "rss/")
  "Directory for local Atom feeds (HN, Reddit, …) under no-littering `var/rss/'.")

(defvar +elfeed-hn-atom
  (expand-file-name "hackernews.atom" +elfeed-local-dir)
  "Path of the HN Atom file written by scripts/hn-elfeed.py.")

(defvar +elfeed-reddit-atom
  (expand-file-name "reddit-emacs.atom" +elfeed-local-dir)
  "Path of the private r/emacs Atom file produced by scripts/reddit-elfeed.py.")

(defun +elfeed-file-feed (filename &rest tags)
  "Build an Elfeed feed entry for local FILENAME under `+elfeed-local-dir' with TAGS."
  (cons (concat "file://" (expand-file-name filename +elfeed-local-dir)) tags))

(defun +elfeed-hn-feed (&rest tags)
  "Build the Hacker News file:// feed entry with TAGS (default hackernews)."
  (cons (concat "file://" +elfeed-hn-atom) (or tags '(hackernews))))

(defun +elfeed-feed-url (feed)
  "Return the URL string of FEED (string or (url . tags) entry)."
  (if (stringp feed) feed (car feed)))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for the ~elfeed~ filters

过滤只用两个标签，但它们是正交的两个维度，所以正则也分两组。~ad~ 只对 ~v2ex.com~ 生效，正则是一
串招聘/机场/广告词；~pr~ 要求「消费品牌 × 上市措辞 − 行业新闻排除词」三个正则同时成立，是给 IT之家
的产品通稿用的。这里用 RSSHub 的「IT 资讯」分类源而不是官网总 RSS，因为官网总 RSS 混入智车之家通稿。

策略是保守的：宁可漏标也不误标，所以每个正则都配了注释说明它防的是什么。
~+elfeed-ad-title-p~/~+elfeed-pr-title-p~ 只是把三个正则的组合写成判定函数，好在 hook 里复用。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
(defvar +elfeed-ithome-url "https://rsshub.rssforever.com/ithome/it"
  "IT之家「IT 资讯」分类源（RSSHub）。官网总 RSS 混入智车之家通稿。")

;; V2EX hard ads: scoped to v2ex.com in `+elfeed-tag-promo-entry'.
(defvar +elfeed-ad-title-regexp
  (rx (or "中转" "机场" "[招聘]" "[接活]" "[广告]" "免费领"))
  "Title regexp for V2EX-style hard ads.")

;; IT之家 product PR: brand × shelf signal, minus industry-news exclusions.
(defvar +elfeed-pr-brand-regexp
  (rx (or "vivo" "OPPO" "iQOO" "真我" "红米" "REDMI" "小米" "荣耀"
          "华为" "一加" "realme" "魅族" "三星"
          "比亚迪" "长城" "魏牌" "奇瑞" "红旗" "吉利" "长安" "一汽"
          "理想" "问界" "小鹏" "蔚来" "极氪" "阿维塔" "零跑"
          "丰田" "本田" "大众" "奔驰" "宝马" "奥迪" "捷豹" "路虎"
          "启境" "深蓝" "方程豹" "腾势" "智己" "飞凡" "iCAR"))
  "Consumer brands used to detect IT之家 product-PR titles.")

(defvar +elfeed-pr-signal-regexp
  (rx (or "规格曝光" "开启预售" "限时权益价" "到手价" "万元起" "元起"
          "预售价" "首发亮相" "高定车色" "车色" "车展"
          "开售" "预售" "上市"))
  "Shelf/launch phrasing that, with a brand, marks a title as PR.")

(defvar +elfeed-pr-exclude-regexp
  (rx (or "专利" "开源" "罢工" "灰度" "推送" "论文" "研究"))
  "If present, the title is treated as industry news rather than PR.")

(defun +elfeed-ad-title-p (title)
  "Return non-nil if TITLE looks like a V2EX hard ad."
  (and (stringp title)
       (let ((case-fold-search t))
         (string-match-p +elfeed-ad-title-regexp title))))

(defun +elfeed-pr-title-p (title)
  "Return non-nil if TITLE looks like an IT之家 product-PR item."
  (and (stringp title)
       (let ((case-fold-search t))
         (and (string-match-p +elfeed-pr-brand-regexp title)
              (string-match-p +elfeed-pr-signal-regexp title)
              (not (string-match-p +elfeed-pr-exclude-regexp title))))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for ~+elfeed-ensure-reddit-feed~

r/emacs 的 atom 是私有的、由脚本生成，可能不存在，所以注册要幂等：文件可读才注册、已在
~elfeed-feeds~ 里就不再 append。比较的是 URL 而不是整条 ~(url . tags)~ 对，于是同一条 feed 换了标签
也算「已经注册过」。

它有两个调用点（~:config~ 与更新子进程的 sentinel），因为 atom 可能在一次成功的 Reddit 抓取之后才
出现——只在一个地方调，第二个场景就会漏。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
(defun +elfeed-ensure-reddit-feed ()
  "Register the Reddit local feed when its atom file is readable.
Safe to call repeatedly; does not duplicate entries."
  (when (file-readable-p +elfeed-reddit-atom)
    (let* ((entry (+elfeed-file-feed "reddit-emacs.atom" 'r/emacs))
           (url (car entry))
           (known (seq-some (lambda (feed)
                              (equal url (+elfeed-feed-url feed)))
                            elfeed-feeds)))
      (unless known
        (setq elfeed-feeds (append elfeed-feeds (list entry)))))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for the ~elfeed~ window rules

窗口这一维是本模块自己的约定，不是通用布局规则，所以它不放进 ~init-emacs-window~：
~+elfeed-display-entry~ 复用右侧已有的 entry 窗口，~elfeed-show-entry-delete~ 设成 ~ignore~，于是
~n~ / ~SPC~ 只换 buffer、不删窗口。删掉之后下一次 ~split-window-sensibly~ 的方向会变。

~+elfeed-hn-llm~ 是一个开关而不是设置：交互更新（搜索 buffer 里 ~g~ / ~G~）总是带
~ELFEED_HN_LLM=1~，两小时一次的后台刷新绝不自动调 Magpie，除非这个变量非 nil。默认 ~nil~ 的意思是
「后台静默花钱」在结构上不可能发生。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
(defun +elfeed-show-window ()
  "Return a live window already showing an Elfeed entry, or nil."
  (cl-find-if (lambda (window)
                (with-current-buffer (window-buffer window)
                  (derived-mode-p 'elfeed-show-mode)))
              (window-list nil 'no-minibuf)))

(defun +elfeed-display-entry (buffer)
  "Show BUFFER to the right of the search list.

Reuse an existing entry window so `n' and `SPC' replace the article
instead of deleting that window and splitting again. Point stays in
the entry window."
  (let* ((search (get-buffer-window (elfeed-search-buffer)))
         (existing (+elfeed-show-window))
         (window (if (window-live-p existing)
                     (progn
                       (set-window-buffer existing buffer)
                       existing)
                   (display-buffer-in-direction
                    buffer
                    `((direction . right)
                      (window . ,(or search (selected-window))))))))
    (when (window-live-p window)
      (select-window window))
    window))

(defvar +elfeed-hn-llm nil
  "Force-enable HN Magpie summarization when non-nil even on background updates.

Interactive `elfeed-update' (search `g') always runs HN generation.
`elfeed-update-background' never does, unless this variable is non-nil.
Shell gate remains ELFEED_HN_LLM (see scripts/update-elfeed-feeds).")
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for ~elfeed~

~use-package~ 主体，篇幅主要花在把上面那些函数接进 elfeed 的回调里，值得单独记的是这条更新链：两条 ~:around~
advice 共用 ~+elfeed--update-after-local-feeds~，只有第三个参数（要不要让子进程生成 HN 摘要）不同。
不能合并成一条、在 advice 里判「现在是谁在调」——nadvice 传进来的 ~fn~ 是包装对象，~eq~ 不到
~#'elfeed-update~。

子进程跑 =scripts/update-elfeed-feeds= 重新生成本地 atom，等它结束后才调原来的更新函数；即使子进程
失败也照样继续拉远端 feed，只在 ~*elfeed-local-feeds*~ 里留一条 message——本地 atom 坏了不该让 RSS
整个停摆。token 从 ~auth-source~ 取（~:host "reddit-private-rss"~），取不到就跳过私有 feed。
~:config~ 里剩下的是数据：feed 列表本身（远端 + 本地）、一个过滤串 ~"@6months +unread -ad -pr"~、
两个挂到 ~elfeed-new-entry-hook~ 上的打标函数，以及一个给已有条目补标的一次性命令
~+elfeed-backfill-promo-tags~。db 与 enclosure 目录交给 no-littering，并把这两棵树排除出 recentf。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~elfeed~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-elfeed/elfeed>
+ 官方手册：<https://elpa.nongnu.org/nongnu-devel/elfeed.html>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
;; [elfeed] Read rss within Emacs
(use-package elfeed
  :straight t
  :init
  (require 'auth-source)

  ;; Local generators rewrite Atom files, then the original update fetches
  ;; file:// + remote feeds.  All local atoms live under var/rss/ (no-littering).
  ;; Interactive update always enables HN; background never calls Magpie unattended.
  ;; Two :around hooks (not one shared) so RUN-HN is reliable — nadvice's
  ;; FN argument is a wrapper object, not eq to #'elfeed-update.
  (defun +elfeed--update-after-local-feeds (fn args run-hn)
    "Refresh local feeds asynchronously, then apply FN to ARGS.
When RUN-HN is non-nil (or `+elfeed-hn-llm'), export ELFEED_HN_LLM=1."
    (if (process-live-p (get-process "elfeed-local-feeds"))
        (progn
          (message "Local feed update is already running; updating remote feeds only")
          (apply fn args))
      (let ((process-environment (copy-sequence process-environment))
            (command (expand-file-name "scripts/update-elfeed-feeds" user-emacs-directory))
            (token (condition-case err
                       (auth-source-pick-first-password :host "reddit-private-rss"
                                                        :port "rss")
                     (error
                      (message "Elfeed: cannot read Reddit RSS token (%s); skipping private feed"
                               (error-message-string err))
                      nil))))
        (when token
          (setenv "REDDIT_PRIVATE_RSS_TOKEN" token))
        (when (or run-hn +elfeed-hn-llm)
          (setenv "ELFEED_HN_LLM" "1"))
        (make-process :name "elfeed-local-feeds"
                      :buffer (get-buffer-create "*elfeed-local-feeds*")
                      :command (list command)
                      :noquery t
                      :sentinel
                      (lambda (process _event)
                        (unless (process-live-p process)
                          (unless (zerop (process-exit-status process))
                            (message "Local feed update failed; see *elfeed-local-feeds*"))
                          ;; Atom may have appeared after a successful Reddit fetch.
                          (+elfeed-ensure-reddit-feed)
                          (apply fn args)))))))

  (defadvice! +elfeed-update-after-local-feeds-a (fn &rest args)
    :around #'elfeed-update
    "Like `+elfeed--update-after-local-feeds' with HN generation enabled."
    (+elfeed--update-after-local-feeds fn args t))

  (defadvice! +elfeed-update-background-after-local-feeds-a (fn &rest args)
    :around #'elfeed-update-background
    "Like `+elfeed--update-after-local-feeds' without HN (unless forced)."
    (+elfeed--update-after-local-feeds fn args nil))

  (defun +elfeed-update-with-hn-llm ()
    "Alias of `elfeed-update' (interactive path always runs HN generation)."
    (interactive)
    (elfeed-update))

  ;; Background refresh: first run ~1 min after init, then every 2 hours.
  ;; Skips HN Magpie summarization — remote/file feeds + optional Reddit only.
  (run-at-time "1 min" (* 60 60 2) #'elfeed-update-background)
  :bind (:map elfeed-search-mode-map
              ;; Elfeed 3.4 and 4.2: g redraws, G fetches.
              ;; `elfeed-search-update--force' is an obsolete alias of
              ;; `revert-buffer' since 4.0.0.  `elfeed-search-fetch'
              ;; calls `elfeed-update', so the local-feed advice still runs.
              ("g" . revert-buffer)
              ("G" . elfeed-search-fetch)
              :map elfeed-show-mode-map
              ("M-v" . scroll-down-command)
              ("j" . scroll-up-line)
              ("k" . scroll-down-line))
  :config
  ;; elfeed-db-directory / elfeed-enclosure-default-dir: leave to no-littering
  ;; (var/elfeed/db/, var/elfeed/enclosures/).  Do not override to config root.
  (make-directory +elfeed-local-dir t)
  (setq elfeed-feeds (list
                      ;; HN atom: var/rss/hackernews.atom (scripts/hn-elfeed.py)
                      (+elfeed-hn-feed 'hackernews)
                      ;; emacs
                      '("https://karthinks.com/index.xml" karthinks)
                      '("https://emacsredux.com/atom.xml" redux)
                      '("https://egh0bww1.com/rss.xml" includeyy)
                      '("https://www.rahuljuliato.com/rss.xml" rahul)
                      ;; r/emacs file feed only if atom exists (see +elfeed-ensure-reddit-feed)
                      ;; programming
                      '("https://matklad.github.io/feed.xml" matklad)
                      '("https://rust-analyzer.github.io/feed.xml" rust-analyzer)
                      '("https://blog.rust-lang.org/feed.xml" rust)
                      ;; news
                      '("https://sspai.com/feed" sspai)
                      '("https://nikonrumors.com/feed/" nikon-rumors)
                      '("https://rss.utgd.net/feed" untaged)
                      '("https://plink.anyfeeder.com/people-daily" people-daily news)
                      ;; tech (IT之家: RSSHub 分类源，不用官网总 RSS)
                      (list +elfeed-ithome-url 'ithome)
                      '("https://feeds.feedburner.com/ruanyifeng" RYF)
                      ;; 知乎日报（RSSHub，知乎官方 rss 已挂）
                      '("https://rsshub.rssforever.com/zhihu/daily" zhihu daily)
                      ;; v2ex
                      '("https://www.v2ex.com/feed/tab/all.xml" v2ex)
                      '("https://www.v2ex.com/feed/tab/tech.xml" v2ex tech)
                      ;; youtube
                      '("https://www.youtube.com/feeds/videos.xml?channel_id=UCz0ONCn6eRcDJGsUzupc3TA" ytb-links)
                      '("https://www.youtube.com/feeds/videos.xml?channel_id=UCVTifvD7WFz1Z-AnEzUoUUA" ytb-fansuki)
                      '("https://www.youtube.com/feeds/videos.xml?channel_id=UCiQo406SKypmtAQXIHdZ6mA" ytb-birchpunk)
                      '("https://www.youtube.com/feeds/videos.xml?channel_id=UCue63vweCtx5j6jylVZsd7w" ytb-hummingbird)
                      '("https://www.youtube.com/feeds/videos.xml?channel_id=UCRewJ9oGONpRm_kaYU6UPGQ" ytb-kolar)
                      '("https://www.youtube.com/feeds/videos.xml?channel_id=UCMZZNUTkXjuWlYyB_RwxNKA" ytb-wsf-xmm))
        ;; Right-hand entry window stays up. `n' / `SPC' replace its buffer
        ;; and keep point there. Deleting the window makes the next article
        ;; split again, and the direction then follows `split-window-sensibly'.
        elfeed-show-entry-switch #'+elfeed-display-entry
        elfeed-show-entry-delete #'ignore
        elfeed-search-title-max-width 100
        elfeed-search-title-min-width 30
        elfeed-search-trailing-width 25
        elfeed-show-truncate-long-urls t
        elfeed-show-unique-buffers t
        ;; Hide V2EX hard ads (`ad') and IT之家 product PR (`pr').
        elfeed-search-filter "@6months +unread -ad -pr")
  (setq-default elfeed-search-filter "@6months +unread -ad -pr")

  (defun +elfeed-entry-feed-url (entry)
    "Return the feed URL of ENTRY, or nil."
    (let ((feed (elfeed-entry-feed entry)))
      (and feed (elfeed-feed-url feed))))

  (defun +elfeed-tag-promo-entry (entry)
    "Tag ENTRY as `ad' (V2EX hard ad) or `pr' (IT之家 product PR)."
    (let* ((title (elfeed-entry-title entry))
           (url (or (+elfeed-entry-feed-url entry) "")))
      (cond
       ((and (string-match-p "v2ex\\.com" url)
             (+elfeed-ad-title-p title))
        (elfeed-tag entry 'ad))
       ((and (string-match-p "ithome" url)
             (+elfeed-pr-title-p title))
        (elfeed-tag entry 'pr)))))

  (defun +elfeed-dedup-ithome-entry (entry)
    "Drop `unread' when ENTRY is an RSSHub IT之家 copy of an older link."
    (let ((url (or (+elfeed-entry-feed-url entry) ""))
          (link (elfeed-entry-link entry)))
      (when (and link
                 (hash-table-p elfeed-db-entries)
                 (string-match-p "rsshub\\..*/ithome" url))
        (catch 'found
          (maphash
           (lambda (_id other)
             (when (and (not (eq other entry))
                        (stringp (elfeed-entry-link other))
                        (string= (elfeed-entry-link other) link))
               (elfeed-untag entry 'unread)
               (throw 'found t)))
           elfeed-db-entries)))))

  (defun +elfeed-backfill-promo-tags ()
    "Apply promo tags to every existing entry and save the database."
    (interactive)
    (let ((n-ad 0)
          (n-pr 0))
      (with-elfeed-db-visit (entry _feed)
        (let ((had-ad (elfeed-tagged-p 'ad entry))
              (had-pr (elfeed-tagged-p 'pr entry)))
          (+elfeed-tag-promo-entry entry)
          (unless had-ad
            (when (elfeed-tagged-p 'ad entry)
              (setq n-ad (1+ n-ad))))
          (unless had-pr
            (when (elfeed-tagged-p 'pr entry)
              (setq n-pr (1+ n-pr))))))
      (elfeed-db-save)
      (when (fboundp 'elfeed-search-update)
        (elfeed-search-update :force))
      (message "Elfeed promo backfill: %d ad, %d pr" n-ad n-pr)
      (list :ad n-ad :pr n-pr)))

  (add-hook 'elfeed-new-entry-hook #'+elfeed-tag-promo-entry)
  (add-hook 'elfeed-new-entry-hook #'+elfeed-dedup-ithome-entry)

  (+elfeed-ensure-reddit-feed)
  (make-directory elfeed-enclosure-default-dir t)

  ;; Exclude db + enclosure trees from recentf (predicate: path-safe).
  (add-to-list 'recentf-exclude
               (lambda (file)
                 (or (file-in-directory-p file elfeed-db-directory)
                     (file-in-directory-p file elfeed-enclosure-default-dir))))
  )
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for ~telega~

README：完整功能的非官方 Telegram 客户端，实现了官方客户端的大部分特性，缺的主要是 voice/video
chat 的完整支持；核心成熟到可以日常聊天。

这一段几乎全是针对上游行为的 hack，值得知道的有五类：图片类选项必须在 ~:init~ 里先设（其它选项在它们
之前就取了默认值）；bot 命令补全上游返回嵌套列表，所以重定义 ~telega-completions--bot-commands~；
赞助消息用 ~:override~ 的 advice 直接返回 nil，再在 ~telega-ready-hook~ 里关掉服务端开关；贴纸靠
advice 临时把 ~telega-use-images~ 绑成 ~t~（全局是 ~nil~，否则图片会影响启动）；~telega-alert~ 的
title/body 里带文本属性，macOS 通知不接受，所以用 ~:filter-args~ 剥掉。
~+telega-completions-setup-capf~ 把每个 CAPF 包在「point 在输入区」的守卫里，否则补全会扫到只读的
历史消息。哪些上游特性故意没抄也写在注释里：Matrix bridge（数据是私人的）、keyword 过滤、
nerd-icons 图标。

块首那行 ~;; -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-~ 要留着：它是文件中段的一行文件局部变量声明，Emacs 只认
文件第一行与第二行的 cookie，所以这一行实际不生效——但它属于代码，删掉就是改代码。
另外两处按平台分叉：=gnu/linux= 在 ~telega-before-auth-hook~ 里加一个本地 SOCKS5 代理，~darwin~ 下
开 ~telega-alert-mode~。

+ 包名（GitHub zevlg/telega.el）：~telega~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/zevlg/telega.el>
+ 官方手册：<https://zevlg.github.io/telega.el/>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
;; -*- lexical-binding: t; -*-
(use-package telega
  :straight (:host github :repo "zevlg/telega.el"
                   :files (:defaults "contrib/*.el" "etc"))
  :require-incrementally
  (password-cache cl-lib find-func emacsbug
                  telega-customize telega-server telega-root telega-ins
                  telega-match telega-filter telega-chat telega-folders
                  telega-user telega-info telega-media telega-sticker
                  telega-util telega-vvnote telega-webpage telega-notifications
                  telega-modes telega-i18n telega-story telega-completions
                  telega-tdlib telega-tdlib-events t)
  :preface
  (defun +telega-install-tdlib ()
    "Fetch and install telega's expected TDLib commit under ~/.local."
    (interactive)
    (unless (eq system-type 'darwin)
      (user-error "+telega-install-tdlib currently only supports macOS"))
    (require 'telega)
    (require 'compile)
    (let ((script (expand-file-name "scripts/install-telega-tdlib" user-emacs-directory))
          (version (telega-dockrefile-tdlib-version))
          (prefix (expand-file-name "~/.local")))
      (unless (file-executable-p script)
        (user-error "TDLib install script is not executable: %s" script))
      (compilation-start
       (mapconcat #'shell-quote-argument (list script version prefix) " ")
       'compilation-mode
       (lambda (_) "*telega-install-tdlib*"))))

  (defun +telega-toggle-archive ()
    "Toggle telega root buffer between the main and archive filters."
    (interactive)
    (let* ((archive-p (equal (telega-filter-active) '(archive)))
           (filter (if archive-p (list telega-filter-default) '(archive))))
      (telega-filters-push filter)
      (message "telega filter: %s" (if archive-p telega-filter-default 'archive))))

  (defun +telega-reaction-emoji (emoji)
    "Return EMOJI, using the emoji presentation for a plain heart."
    (if (equal emoji "❤") "❤️" emoji))

  (defun +telega-reaction-title (title)
    "Use the emoji presentation when TITLE starts with a plain heart."
    (if (and (stringp title) (string-prefix-p "❤" title))
        (concat "❤️" (substring title 1))
      title))

  (defun +telega-alert--strip-text-properties (args)
    "Strip text properties from the notification spec in ARGS.
`alert' embeds the title and body in a notifier string, where Emacs
text properties are invalid."
    (let ((notify-spec (copy-sequence (car args))))
      (dolist (key '(:title :body))
        (let ((text (plist-get notify-spec key)))
          (when (stringp text)
            (setq notify-spec
                  (plist-put notify-spec key
                             (substring-no-properties text))))))
      (list notify-spec)))

  (defun +telega-save-file-to-clipboard (msg)
    "Save the file attached to MSG onto the system clipboard."
    (interactive (list (telega-msg-for-interactive)))
    (let ((file (telega-msg--content-file msg)))
      (unless file
        (user-error "No file associated with message"))
      (telega-file--download file
        :priority 32
        :update-callback
        (lambda (dfile)
          (telega-msg-redisplay msg)
          (when (telega-file--downloaded-p dfile)
            (let* ((fpath (telega-file--path dfile))
                   (command
                    (if (eq system-type 'darwin)
                        (list "osascript" "-e"
                              (format "set the clipboard to POSIX file %S" fpath))
                      (list "sh" "-c" (format "wl-copy < %s" (shell-quote-argument fpath))))))
              (make-process
               :name "telega-clipboard"
               :buffer nil
               :command command)))))))

  (defun +telega-msg-save-to-cloud-copyleft (msg)
    "Send the file attached to MSG to Saved Messages."
    (interactive (list (telega-msg-for-interactive)))
    (let ((file (telega-msg--content-file msg)))
      (unless file
        (user-error "No file associated with message"))
      (telega-file--download file
        :priority 32
        :update-callback
        (lambda (dfile)
          (telega-msg-redisplay msg)
          (when (telega-file--downloaded-p dfile)
            (let* ((fpath (telega-file--path dfile))
                   (ifile (telega-chatbuf--gen-input-file fpath 'Document)))
              (telega--sendMessage
               (telega-chat-me t)
               (list :@type "inputMessageDocument"
                     :document (list :@type "inputDocument"
                                     :document ifile)
                     :caption (telega-fmt-text "#copyleft")))
              (message "Saved to cloud: %s" (file-name-nondirectory fpath))))))))

  (defun +telega-redraw-for-theme (&rest _)
    "Redraw telega buffers so palette colors follow the enabled theme."
    (when (and (boundp 'telega-root-buffer-name)
               (get-buffer telega-root-buffer-name))
      (telega-root-view--redisplay)
      (dolist (chatbuf (telega-chat-buffers))
        (with-current-buffer chatbuf
          (when telega-chatbuf--ewoc
            (with-telega-buffer-modify
             (telega-save-cursor
               (telega-chatbuf--footer-update)
               (ewoc-refresh telega-chatbuf--ewoc))))))))

  :init
  ;; Read while the other image options take their defaults.
  (setq telega-use-images nil)
  :custom-face
  (telega-msg-heading ((t (:inherit hl-line :background unspecified))))
  (telega-msg-inline-reply ((t (:inherit (hl-line font-lock-function-name-face)))))
  (telega-msg-inline-forward ((t (:inherit (hl-line font-lock-type-face)))))
  (telega-msg-self-title ((t (:bold t :italic t))))
  :bind (:map telega-chat-button-map
              ("h" . nil)
              :map telega-root-mode-map
              ("A" . +telega-toggle-archive)
              :map telega-prefix-map
              ("p" . telega-chatbuf-filter-search)
              ("d" . telega-chat-remove-member)
              ("m" . telega-describe-chat-members)
              :map telega-msg-button-map
              ("C" . +telega-save-file-to-clipboard)
              ("s" . +telega-msg-save-to-cloud-copyleft))
  :hook ((telega-chat-mode . corfu-mode)
         (telega-chat-mode . +telega-completions-setup-capf))
  :config
  (setq telega-root-fill-column 70
        telega-chat-fill-column 90)

  (add-hook! telega-chat-mode-hook
    (defun +telega-disable-visual-fill-column-h ()
      "Disable visual fill column in Telega chat buffers."
      (visual-fill-column-mode -1)))

  (telega-root-auto-fill-mode -1)
  (remove-hook 'telega-chat-mode-hook #'telega-chat-auto-fill-mode)

  (setq telega-chat-show-avatars nil
        telega-user-show-avatars nil
        telega-root-show-avatars nil
        telega-completions-username-show-avatars nil
        telega-active-locations-show-avatars nil
        telega-avatar-text-function (lambda (&rest _) "")

        telega-translate-to-language-by-default "zh"
        telega-chat-input-markups '("markdown2" "org")
        telega-server-libs-prefix (expand-file-name "~/.local")

        ;; root page
        telega-chat-button-width '(0.2 10 25)
        telega-brackets '(((chat (return t)) "" "")
                          ((user (return t)) "" ""))
        telega-chat-button-format-plist (list :with-title 'full-name
                                              :with-username-p nil
                                              :with-title-faces-p nil
                                              :with-unread-trail-p t
                                              :with-members-trail-p nil
                                              :with-bot-verification-p nil
                                              :with-status-icons-trail-p t)

        ;; emoji
        telega-symbol-pin "%"
        telega-symbol-folder ""
        telega-symbol-photo ""
