        ;; Avoid Telega's explicit `compose-chars' strings.
        telega-topic-brackets '("⟦" "⟧")
        telega-symbol-forward "→"
        telega-symbol-story-reply "↩"

        ;; filters
        telega-filters-custom nil
        telega-filter-custom-show-folders nil

        ;; images.  `telega-use-images' is set in :init, before these
        ;; options take their defaults.
        telega-emoji-use-images nil
        telega-symbols-emojify
        (cl-reduce (lambda (emojify key)
                     (assq-delete-all key emojify))
                   '(checkmark heavy-checkmark button-close)
                   :initial-value (copy-sequence
                                   (default-value 'telega-symbols-emojify)))

        telega-date-format-alist '((today . "%H:%M") (this-week . "%m/%d") (old . "%m/%d") (date . "%y/%m/%d")
                                   (time . "%H:%M") (date-time . "%y/%m/%d. %H:%M") (date-long . "%y/%m/%d")
                                   (date-break-bar . "%m/%d"))
        telega-chat-group-messages-timespan 600
        telega-completions-capf-functions '(telega-capf-username
                                            telega-capf-hashtag
                                            telega-capf-markdown-precode
                                            telega-capf-botcmd))

  (defun +telega-capf-in-input (capf)
    "Call CAPF only while point is in Telega's writable input area."
    (when (and (markerp telega-chatbuf--input-marker)
               (>= (point) telega-chatbuf--input-marker)
               (not (get-text-property (point) 'read-only)))
      (funcall capf)))

  (defun +telega-completions-setup-capf ()
    "Set up Telega CAPFs without completing read-only message text."
    (telega-completions-setup-capf)
    (setq-local completion-at-point-functions
                (mapcar (lambda (capf)
                          (apply-partially #'+telega-capf-in-input capf))
                        telega-completions-capf-functions)))

  (when (eq system-type 'gnu/linux)
    (add-hook! telega-before-auth-hook
      (defun +telega-enable-linux-proxy-h ()
        "Add and enable the local SOCKS5 proxy before Telega authorizes."
        (telega--addProxy '(:server "127.0.0.1" :port 7891 :type (:@type "proxyTypeSocks5"))
          :enable-p t
          :comment "Local SOCKS5 proxy"))))

  ;; HACK: Work around upstream bot command completion returning nested lists.
  ;; Each mapped candidate list is freshly allocated, so `mapcan' is safe here.
  (defun telega-completions--bot-commands (chat)
    "Return bot command completion candidates for CHAT."
    (let* ((info (telega-chat--info chat))
           (telega-full-info-offline-p nil)
           (full-info (telega--full-info info)))
      (if (telega-chatbuf-match-p '(type bot))
          (telega-completions--bot-commands-list
           (telega--tl-get full-info :bot_info :commands))
        (mapcan (lambda (bot-commands)
                  (telega-completions--bot-commands-list
                   (plist-get bot-commands :commands)
                   (telega-msg-sender-username
                    (telega-user-get
                     (plist-get bot-commands :bot_user_id))
                    'with-@)))
                (plist-get full-info :bot_commands)))))

  ;; HACK: Show full name only in chatbuf
  (defadvice! +telega-message-header-username-only-a
    (orig msg &optional msg-chat msg-sender addon-inserter)
    :around #'telega-ins--message-header
    (let* ((msg (copy-sequence msg))
           (orig-ins (symbol-function 'telega-ins--msg-sender)))
      (setq msg (plist-put msg :author_signature nil))
      (setq msg (plist-put msg :sender_tag nil))
      (setq msg (plist-put msg :sender_boost_count 0))
      (cl-letf (((symbol-function 'telega-ins--msg-sender)
                 (lambda (sender &rest _args)
                   (funcall orig-ins sender
                            :with-title 'full-name
                            :with-username-p nil
                            :with-badges-p nil)))
                ((symbol-function 'telega-chat-admin-get)
                 (lambda (&rest _) nil)))
        (funcall orig msg msg-chat msg-sender addon-inserter))))

  ;; HACK: show stickers
  (defadvice! +telega-enable-image-for-stickers (orig-fn &rest args)
    :around '(telega-sticker--create-image
              telega-describe-stickerset
              telega-ins--sticker-list
              telega-ins--sticker-image
              telega-ins--inline-sticker
              telega-chatbuf-sticker-insert)
    (let ((telega-use-images t))
      (apply orig-fn args)))

  ;; HACK: disable sponsored msg
  (defadvice! +telega-hide-sponsored-messages-a (&rest _)
    :override #'telega-chatbuf-footer-ins-sponsored-messages
    nil)

  (add-hook! telega-ready-hook
    (defun +telega-disable-sponsored-messages-h ()
      (telega--toggleHasSponsoredMessagesEnabled nil)))

  ;; HACK: remove filter line in main filter
  (defadvice! +telega-hide-default-filter-footer-a (orig-fn &rest args)
    :around #'telega-filters--footer
    (if (and (telega-filter-default-p)
             (not telega--sort-criteria)
             (not telega--sort-inverted))
        ""
      (apply orig-fn args)))

  ;; HACK: preview stickers with vertico
  (defadvice! +telega-stickerset-preview-with-vertico-a (fn &rest args)
    :around #'telega-stickerset--minibuf-post-command
    (cl-letf (((symbol-function #'completion-all-sorted-completions)
               (lambda (&rest _)
                 (list (vertico--candidate)))))
      (apply fn args)))

  ;; Lucius-style chat chrome.  His Matrix bridge, notification keyword
  ;; filter, and nerd-icons symbols are not copied: the bridge data is
  ;; personal, and nerd-icons is not part of this telega install.
  (keymap-global-set "C-c t" telega-prefix-map)
  (setq telega-autoplay-mode t
        telega-notifications-mode t
        telega-open-file-function #'org-open-file
        telega-sticker-size '(6 . 24)
        telega-symbol-mark (propertize " " 'face 'telega-button-highlight)
        telega-msg-save-dir "~/Downloads"
        telega-root-default-view-function 'telega-view-folders
        telega-chat-folders-insexp (lambda () nil)
        telega-symbol-rich-text-table-border '(?─ ?\s ?\s)
        telega-builtin-palettes-alist
        '((light
           ((:outline "#b4637a") (:foreground "#b4637a") (:background "#d1c7c7"))
           ((:outline "#ea9d34") (:foreground "#ea9d34") (:background "#d1ccc7"))
           ((:outline "#907aa9") (:foreground "#907aa9") (:background "#cdc7d1"))
           ((:outline "#568D68") (:foreground "#568D68") (:background "#c7d1c7"))
           ((:outline "#286983") (:foreground "#286983") (:background "#cacfcf"))
           ((:outline "#56949f") (:foreground "#56949f") (:background "#c6cbd1"))
           ((:outline "#d7827e") (:foreground "#d7827e") (:background "#d1c7cb")))
          (dark
           ((:outline "#eb6f92") (:foreground "#eb6f92") (:background "#3d2828"))
           ((:outline "#f6c177") (:foreground "#f6c177") (:background "#2c2620"))
           ((:outline "#b294bb") (:foreground "#b294bb") (:background "#2e1e2e"))
           ((:outline "#95b1ac") (:foreground "#95b1ac") (:background "#1e2e1e"))
           ((:outline "#81a2be") (:foreground "#81a2be") (:background "#234242"))
           ((:outline "#9ccfd8") (:foreground "#9ccfd8") (:background "#1e262e"))
           ((:outline "#ebbcba") (:foreground "#ebbcba") (:background "#470528"))))
        telega-box-button-styles
        (let ((styles (copy-tree telega-box-button-styles)))
          (dolist (tag '(owner-sender-tag admin-sender-tag))
            (plist-put (alist-get tag styles) :passive-face 'telega-shadow))
          styles))
  (add-hook 'enable-theme-functions #'+telega-redraw-for-theme)
  (add-hook 'telega-msg-ignore-predicates
            (telega-match-gen-predicate 'msg '(sender is-blocked)))
  (advice-add 'telega-ins--msg-reaction-type :around
              (lambda (fn rt)
                (if (eq (intern (plist-get rt :@type)) 'reactionTypeEmoji)
                    (telega-ins (+telega-reaction-emoji (plist-get rt :emoji)))
                  (funcall fn rt))))
  (advice-add 'telega-msg-reaction-title-for-completion :filter-return
              #'+telega-reaction-title)
  (require 'telega-mnz)
  (global-telega-mnz-mode 1)
  (when (eq system-type 'darwin)
    (require 'telega-alert)
    (advice-add 'telega-alert--notify :filter-args
                #'+telega-alert--strip-text-properties)
    (telega-alert-mode 1))
  )

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for ~telega-dired-dwim~

=telega.el= 自带的 contrib（recipe 里的 ~:files~ 装了 =contrib/*.el=），Commentary 一句话说清：它
advice 了 ~dired-do-copy~，把 dired 里标记的文件附到可见的 chatbuf 里——在 dired 里标记文件按 ~C~，
文件就发给那个聊天。

这里没有配置项，~use-package~ 只表达加载策略：~:after telega~ 让它跟着 telega 加载，~:demand t~
让它在那一刻真的加载，而不是等 dired 里第一次按 ~C~ 才靠 autoload 拉起——那条 advice 必须已经装好，
否则 ~C~ 就是普通的复制。

+ 包名（GitHub zevlg/telega.el）：~telega-dired-dwim~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/zevlg/telega.el>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package telega-dired-dwim
  :after telega
  :demand t)
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for ~chirp~

README：Emacs 里的 X/Twitter 浏览器，认证与取数全部交给 ~twitter-cli~，Chirp 自己只做 buffer：
只读视图用 ~special-mode~、图片用 ~image-mode~、撰写 buffer 可编辑。

十一个 ~:commands~ 是它全部的入口，都是延后加载。~:init~ 先做两件事：缓存目录与草稿目录都改到
no-littering 的 =var/chirp/= 下，并 ~make-directory~——首次用之前就得存在。~:config~ 是一串「关掉」：
不显头像、不显媒体、不要分隔符，同 ~telega~ 那边「图片会影响启动」是同一个思路。

+ 包名（GitHub LuciusChen/chirp）：~chirp~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/LuciusChen/chirp>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
;; [chirp] twitter client
(use-package chirp
  :straight (:host github :repo "LuciusChen/chirp")
  :commands (chirp-home
             chirp-following
             chirp-bookmarks
             chirp-likes
             chirp-me
             chirp-list
             chirp-search
             chirp-thread
             chirp-profile
             chirp-profile-followers
             chirp-profile-following-users)
  :init
  (setq chirp-cache-directory (no-littering-expand-var-file-name "chirp/")
        chirp-compose-temporary-directory
        (no-littering-expand-var-file-name "chirp/compose/"))
  (make-directory chirp-cache-directory t)
  (make-directory chirp-compose-temporary-directory t)
  :config
  (setq chirp-show-avatars nil
        chirp-show-tweet-media nil
        chirp-tweet-separator ""
        chirp-tweet-separator-indent 0))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for ~browse-url~

内置包，没有 README；据 =browse-url.el= 的 Commentary：它从 minibuffer 读一个 URL（默认取 point
处的），交给 web 浏览器；另有 ~browse-url-at-point~、~browse-url-of-buffer~ 等入口。

两个键：~C-, o~ = ~browse-url-at-point~（把 point 处的 URL 交给系统浏览器）、~C-, e~ =
~browse-url-emacs~（用 Emacs 自己的 ~url-retrieve~ 抓下来）。本配置没有设
~browse-url-browser-function~，「用哪个外部浏览器」完全交给系统默认。

+ 包名（Emacs 内置）：~browse-url~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-mirror/emacs>（源码随 Emacs 自带）

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
;; [browse-url] Pass a URL to browser
(use-package browse-url
  :bind (("C-, o" . browse-url-at-point)
         ("C-, e" . browse-url-emacs)))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for ~eww~

内置包，没有 README；~eww~ 的命令文档（docstring）说得最简洁：抓一个 URL 并把页面渲染出来，输入
不像 URL 或域名时改用 ~eww-search-prefix~ 去搜。

只有一行设置：~shr-max-image-proportion 0.5~，图片不超过半屏。三个浏览器入口的分工是：读文档用
~eww~、需要 JS 的页面用 ~xwidget~、其余交给系统浏览器。

+ 包名（Emacs 内置）：~eww~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-mirror/emacs>（源码随 Emacs 自带）

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
;; [eww] Builtin browser
(use-package eww
  :config
  (setq shr-max-image-proportion 0.5))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section for ~xwidget~

内置包，没有 README；Commentary 直接指向手册两节——~(emacs)Embedded WebKit Widgets~ 讲用户可见的
功能，~(elisp)Embedded Native Widgets~ 讲 API。

只改 buffer 名格式（~*XWidget: <title>*~）。它是编译期特性：本机这个 Emacs 带 xwidget 支持，
~xwidget-webkit-browse-url~ 才存在。

+ 包名（Emacs 内置）：~xwidget~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/emacs-mirror/emacs>（源码随 Emacs 自带）

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package xwidget
  :config
  (setq xwidget-webkit-buffer-name-format "*XWidget: %T*"))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-web.el= section to provide the feature

~provide~ 把 ~init-emacs-web~ 登记成 feature，这个模块的加载到此结束。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-web.el :mkdirp yes
(provide 'init-emacs-web)
#+end_src

** The =init-emacs-ai.el= module

两条栈并存，因为它们回答不同的问题：~gptel~ 面向「对某段文本发一次请求」，请求、响应、渲染都在
当前 buffer 或一个结果 buffer 里，生命周期随请求结束；~agent-shell~ 面向「一个有状态的命令行会话」
（ACP 协议，会话可恢复、可 fork、可 steer）。键位、数据目录、失败模式都不同，所以不是替代关系——
~C-c g~ 下可以同时看到两组。两块共用的只有一样东西：模型 id 来自本机 Magpie 网关的 picker。

*** The =init-emacs-ai.el= section for ~gptel~ and ~gptel-agent~

~gptel~ README：简单的 LLM 聊天客户端，支持多模型与多后端，「按 Emacs 的精神工作——在任何 buffer
里随时可用」。

backend 是三处约束的合成：~gptel-make-openai~ 指向本机 Magpie 网关（~127.0.0.1:3425~，~http~）——
host 不是 ~api.openai.com~ 所以走 =/v1/chat/completions=；~:stream t~ 必须显式给，否则不流式；
~:models~ 里没列出的模型会被丢掉。用 ~setq-default~ 是因为 ~gptel-backend~ 是 buffer-local。
模型 id 不在本文件里定：~+gptel-magpie-store~ 是本机 Magpie（一个 WebKit 应用）的
~localstorage.sqlite3~ 路径，~+gptel-magpie-model~ 用 ~sqlite3 -readonly … | xxd -r -p |
iconv -f UTF-16LE~ 把值从 UTF-16LE blob 里解出来，读不到就退回 ~gptel-model~。
=auto/deepseek-flash= 不是 picker 的选择，而是 WorkBuddy 自己的 model id（gptel 不跟随 picker）。
两个都定义在 ~:init~ 而不是 ~:config~：下一个包的配置要读它们，而那时 ~gptel~ 可能还没加载。

~gptel-agent~ README：一组 tools 与 prompts，让 ~gptel~ 以「agent」方式用任意 LLM 自主完成任务——
它能访问 web（搜索与抓 URL）、本地文件（读/写/改）、Emacs 的状态（文档与求值）以及 bash；默认除 web
搜索、抓 URL 与读本地文件外都需要确认，也可以把任务委派给不共享上下文的 sub-agent。

本配置只调一次 ~gptel-agent-update~：tools 的注册归它自己管，这里不逐个开关工具，也不放宽上面那层
确认默认值。

+ 包名（MELPA）：~gptel~
+ 包名（MELPA）：~gptel-agent~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/karthink/gptel>（~gptel~）
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/karthink/gptel-agent>（~gptel-agent~）

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
;;; -*- lexical-binding: t -*-

(use-package gptel
  :straight t
  :init
  (setq gptel-model 'auto/deepseek-flash
        gptel-default-mode 'org-mode
        gptel-confirm-tool-calls nil)
  ;; Pi reads Magpie's menu-bar picker from WebKit local storage.
  ;; `auto' is not that choice: it is WorkBuddy's own model id and
  ;; always resolves there.  Defined in :init so agent-shell can read
  ;; it without loading gptel.  gptel does not follow the picker.
  (defvar +gptel-magpie-store
    (expand-file-name
     (concat "Library/WebKit/com.yetone.magpie/WebsiteData/Default/"
             "W7EjdJuyWhjGnN4UoO9On1R3uVoFOb07LV8R479DUDo/"
             "W7EjdJuyWhjGnN4UoO9On1R3uVoFOb07LV8R479DUDo/"
             "LocalStorage/localstorage.sqlite3")
     "~"))

  (defun +gptel-magpie-model ()
    "Return the model currently chosen in Magpie's gateway picker.
WebKit stores the value as a UTF-16LE blob.  Fall back to whatever
`gptel-model' already is when the store cannot be read."
    (let ((chosen (string-trim
                   (shell-command-to-string
                    (format "sqlite3 -readonly %s %s | xxd -r -p | iconv -f UTF-16LE"
                            (shell-quote-argument +gptel-magpie-store)
                            (shell-quote-argument
                             "select hex(value) from ItemTable where key='magpie.model'"))))))
      (if (string-empty-p chosen)
          gptel-model
        (intern chosen))))
  :config
  ;; A host other than api.openai.com uses /v1/chat/completions.
  ;; Streaming is ignored unless the backend sets :stream, and a model
  ;; missing from :models is dropped.  gptel-backend is buffer-local.
  (setq-default gptel-backend
                (gptel-make-openai "Magpie"
                  :host "127.0.0.1:3425"
                  :protocol "http"
                  :stream t
                  :key (lambda () "magpie")
                  :models '(auto/deepseek-flash)))
  (add-hook! gptel-post-stream-hook #'gptel-auto-scroll)
  (add-hook! gptel-post-response-functions #'gptel-end-of-response))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package gptel-agent
  :straight t
  :after gptel
  :config (gptel-agent-update))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-ai.el= section for ~gptel-magit~

README：用 ~gptel~ 给 Magit 加 LLM 功能。这个仓库是 ragnard/gptel-magit 的活跃 fork（roife），
fork 说明里列了新加的东西：可选的 commit style、rationale 输入、流式生成 commit message、解释 diff、
按包选 backend/model。

recipe 指向 roife 的 fork 而不是 MELPA，原因就是上面那些上游没有的功能。~:hook~ 把它装进
~magit-mode~，~:config~ 里定下 body 长度与 commit prompt（取 ~gptel-magit-commit-styles-alist~ 里的
~Conventional Commits~）。~+gptel-magit-fish~ 是本配置自己加的入口，全文没有调用点：它给外部调用方
（参数是一个 FIFO 路径）用——生成成功就往 FIFO 写 ~"0"~ 加 message，失败写 ~"1"~ 加状态或错误，做法
是往 gptel 的 ~:post~ 里追加一个写 FIFO 的回调，不碰生成流程本身。

+ 包名（GitHub roife/gptel-magit）：~gptel-magit~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/roife/gptel-magit>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package gptel-magit
  :straight (gptel-magit :type git :host github :repo "roife/gptel-magit")
  :require-incrementally (magit t)
  :hook ((magit-mode . gptel-magit-install))
  :config
  (setq gptel-magit-body-length 72
        gptel-magit-commit-prompt (cdr (assoc "Conventional Commits" gptel-magit-commit-styles-alist)))

  (defun +gptel-magit-fish (fifo insertp)
    "Generate a message, optionally insert it, and report through FIFO."
    (let ((commit-buffer (magit-commit-message-buffer))
          record fsm)
      (cond
       ((and insertp (not commit-buffer))
        (user-error "No commit in progress"))
       ((and (not insertp) commit-buffer)
        (user-error "Commit already in progress")))
      (setq fsm
            (gptel-magit--generate
             (lambda (message)
               (setq record (concat "0" message)))
             nil commit-buffer "Generating commit message..."))
      (let ((info (gptel-fsm-info fsm)))
        (plist-put
         info :post
         (append
          (plist-get info :post)
          (list
           (lambda (request-info)
             (with-temp-buffer
               (insert
                (or record
                    (if (eq (gptel-fsm-state fsm) 'DONE)
                        "0"
                      (concat "1" (format "%s"
                                          (or (plist-get request-info :status)
                                              (plist-get request-info :error)
                                              "generation failed"))))))
               (write-region (point-min) (point-max)
                             fifo nil 'silent)))))))
      fsm)))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-ai.el= section for ~gptel-quick~

README：一个命令的日常小工具——用 LLM 弹出 point 处的词、活动区域或整段文本的简短解释/摘要；
popup 里 ~+~ 要更长的版本、~M-w~ 复制、~q~ 关掉。

上游（roife 的 fork）用 posframe 弹窗，这里换成了 render buffer：~+gptel-quick-render-function~ 默认
~'buffer~，结果流式写进可复用的 ~*gptel-quick: <action>*~ buffer，方向交给
~split-window-sensibly~（横屏左右分、竖屏上下分；不用 ~display-buffer-below-selected~，那会强制
上下分）。能工作的前提有两个：流式时每个 chunk 是一次 ~stringp~ 回调、结束时回调 ~t~；非流式路径
（~gptel-curl--sentinel~）只回调一次、不发完成信号，所以那里要自己调 ~+gptel-quick--show-buffer~。
reasoning 与 tool 的 chunk 是 cons 而不是 ~nil~，直接忽略——只有 ~nil~ 才表示失败。

四个命令由 ~+gptel-quick-define-command~ 生成（explain / translate / summarize / dict），键位在
~C-c g~ 下，另外在 ~embark~ 的 general/region map 里各挂一个；~dict~ 的 prompt 是一份词典格式模板，
多行字符串因此是顶格的。token 上限由「文本长度的平方根 + 2.5 × 词数」现算，而不是直接用
~gptel-quick-word-count~：buffer 路径刻意让 ~gptel-quick~ 保持惰性加载，那个 defcustom 可能还没加载。

+ 包名（GitHub roife/gptel-quick）：~gptel-quick~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/roife/gptel-quick>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package gptel-quick
  :straight (gptel-quick :type git :host github :repo "roife/gptel-quick")
  :bind (("C-c g e" . +gptel-quick-explain)
         ("C-c g t" . +gptel-quick-translate-to-chinese)
         ("C-c g s" . +gptel-quick-summarize)
         ("C-c g d" . +gptel-quick-dict))
  :preface
  ;; [render] Local addition: stream responses into a reusable side-window
  ;; buffer instead of the fork's posframe popup (see
  ;; dev-docs/notes/gptel-render-non-popup-requirements-2026-02-11.md).
  (defvar +gptel-quick-render-function 'buffer
    "Where `+gptel-quick-region-or-buffer' renders results.
`buffer' streams the response into a reusable \"*gptel-quick:
<action>*\" buffer displayed in a side window below.  `popup' keeps
the roife/gptel-quick posframe popup.")

  (defun +gptel-quick--buffer-name (action)
    "Return the result buffer name for ACTION."
    (format "*gptel-quick: %s*" action))

  (defun +gptel-quick--setup-result-buffer (action source)
    "Return the result buffer for ACTION, documenting SOURCE.
Reuse the buffer across rounds, clearing stale contents and setting
up the header line, major mode and word wrapping."
    (let ((buffer (get-buffer-create (+gptel-quick--buffer-name action))))
      (with-current-buffer buffer
        (erase-buffer)
        (if (fboundp 'markdown-ts-mode)
            (markdown-ts-mode)
          (text-mode))
        (visual-line-mode 1)
        (setq header-line-format
              (format "gptel-quick %s · from %s" action source)))
      buffer))

  (defun +gptel-quick--insert-chunk (buffer chunk)
    "Append CHUNK at the end of the result BUFFER."
    (with-current-buffer buffer
      (goto-char (point-max))
      (insert chunk)))

  (defun +gptel-quick--show-buffer (buffer)
    "Display the result BUFFER, keeping focus.
Reuse an existing window when one already shows BUFFER; otherwise let
`split-window-sensibly' pick the direction from the window size
(`split-window-preferred-direction' defaults to `longest' in Emacs 31:
landscape frames split left-right, portrait frames top-bottom)."
    ;; NOTE: `display-buffer-below-selected' would force a top-bottom
    ;; split on every frame, including wide ones.
    (display-buffer buffer
                    '((display-buffer-reuse-window
                       display-buffer-pop-up-window))))

  (defun +gptel-quick--display-buffer-callback (response info)
    "gptel-request callback rendering RESPONSE into the result buffer.
INFO is gptel's request info; its :context is (ACTION SOURCE-BUFFER-NAME)
as passed by `+gptel-quick-region-or-buffer'.  Append RESPONSE when it
is a streaming chunk, display the buffer once the stream ends
(RESPONSE is t), and report quietly on failure."
    (let* ((context (plist-get info :context))
           (action (or (nth 0 context) 'quick))
           (source (or (nth 1 context) "?"))
           (buffer (or (get-buffer (+gptel-quick--buffer-name action))
                       (+gptel-quick--setup-result-buffer action source))))
      (pcase response
        ((pred stringp)
         (+gptel-quick--insert-chunk buffer response)
         ;; NOTE: curl's non-streaming path calls the callback once with
         ;; the whole response and never sends the `t' completion signal
         ;; (see `gptel-curl--sentinel'), so show here when INFO says the
         ;; request did not stream.
         (unless (plist-get info :stream)
           (+gptel-quick--show-buffer buffer)
           (message "gptel-quick: %s response is in %s"
                    action (buffer-name buffer))))
        ('t
         (+gptel-quick--show-buffer buffer)
         (message "gptel-quick: %s response is in %s" action (buffer-name buffer)))
        ((pred null)
         (message "gptel-quick failed: %s"
                  (or (plist-get info :error) (plist-get info :status) "unknown")))
        ;; NOTE: reasoning and tool chunks arrive as cons cells
        ;; (streaming deepseek models send `reasoning' chunks).  Only
        ;; nil means failure, so these are silently skipped.
        (_ nil))))

  (defun +gptel-quick-region-or-buffer (system-message &optional limit-response thing action)
    "Run `gptel-quick' on THING, the active region, or the buffer.
Preserve SYSTEM-MESSAGE when requesting another response with `+'.  When
LIMIT-RESPONSE is non-nil, apply gptel-quick's count-derived token limit.
Render into the ACTION result buffer when `+gptel-quick-render-function'
is `buffer'."
    (let* ((query-text
            (if thing
                (or (thing-at-point thing t)
                    (user-error "No %s at point" thing))
              (if (use-region-p)
                  (buffer-substring-no-properties (region-beginning) (region-end))
                (buffer-substring-no-properties (point-min) (point-max)))))
           (action (or action 'quick)))
      (when (string-empty-p query-text)
        (user-error "Buffer is empty"))
      (if (eq +gptel-quick-render-function 'popup)
          (progn
            (require 'gptel-quick)
            (gptel-quick query-text nil
                         (append (list :system system-message)
                                 (unless limit-response
                                   (list :max-tokens nil)))))
        ;; NOTE: mirror gptel-quick's count-derived token cap; the
        ;; buffer path keeps gptel-quick lazy-loaded, so the cap
        ;; applies only once its defcustom has been loaded.
        ;; NOTE: the popup path pulls gptel in through `gptel-quick';
        ;; the buffer path calls `gptel-request' directly, so make sure
        ;; gptel itself is loaded first.
        (unless (fboundp 'gptel-request)
          (require 'gptel))
        (let ((gptel-max-tokens
               (when (and limit-response (boundp 'gptel-quick-word-count))
                 (floor (+ (sqrt (length query-text))
                           (* 2.5 gptel-quick-word-count))))))
          (+gptel-quick--setup-result-buffer action (buffer-name))
          (gptel-request query-text
            :system system-message
            ;; NOTE: `gptel-request' defaults :stream to nil, and a
            ;; non-streaming run never sends the `t' completion
            ;; signal that displays the buffer.  gptel still gates
            ;; this on `gptel-stream', `gptel-use-curl' and the
            ;; backend's own :stream flag.
            :stream t
            :context (list action (buffer-name))
            :callback #'+gptel-quick--display-buffer-callback)))))

  (defmacro +gptel-quick-define-command (name doc prompt &optional limit thing action)
    "Define NAME as a gptel-quick action over the region or buffer."
    `(defun ,name ()
       ,doc (interactive)
       (+gptel-quick-region-or-buffer ,prompt ,limit ,thing ,action)))

  (+gptel-quick-define-command +gptel-quick-explain
                               "Explain the active region, or the whole buffer, in Chinese."
                               "Explain in clear Chinese, preserving necessary context and details." t nil 'explain)

  (+gptel-quick-define-command +gptel-quick-translate-to-chinese
                               "Translate the active region, or the whole buffer, to Chinese."
                               "Translate into fluent Chinese." nil nil 'translate)

  (+gptel-quick-define-command +gptel-quick-summarize
                               "Summarize the active region, or the whole buffer, in Chinese."
                               "Summarize in Chinese while preserving details and key information." t nil 'summarize)

  (+gptel-quick-define-command +gptel-quick-dict
                               "Explain the word at point in dictionary style."
                               "Given a word, explain it in the style of a concise English dictionary entry,
and add accurate Chinese translations for each sense. Preserve the compact dictionary
format in plain text rather than giving a long explanatory article or markdown document.
No need for chinese in sentences.

Use this format:
*word* syllable division | pronunciation | part of speech (inflections)

1. English definition **中文**
     *Example sentence.*
 | Sub-sense or extended meaning **中文**
     *Example sentence.*
• ...
2. English definition **中文**
     *Example sentence.*"
                               nil 'word 'dict)

  (with-eval-after-load 'embark
    (keymap-set embark-general-map "?" #'gptel-quick)
    (keymap-set embark-region-map "E" #'+gptel-quick-explain)
    (keymap-set embark-region-map "T" #'+gptel-quick-translate-to-chinese)
    (keymap-set embark-region-map "S" #'+gptel-quick-summarize)
    (keymap-set embark-region-map "D" #'+gptel-quick-dict))

  :config
  (setq gptel-quick-word-count 50
        gptel-quick-timeout nil))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-ai.el= section for ~agent-shell~

README：把 AI agent 带进 Emacs——熟悉的 buffer 和键位，没有终端模拟，全部走 ACP（Agent Client
Protocol），支持 24 个 agent。

~:init~ 里那一串 ~setq~ 大多是「关」：不要 context sources、不要 MCP、不要 welcome 消息、不要
chat-mode、表格不加斑马纹；~agent-shell-agent-configs~ 只留一个构造函数
~+agent-shell-pi-make-agent-config~，它包住上游的 pi config，把 ~:default-model-id~ 换成读 Magpie
picker 的函数（~+agent-shell-pi-model-id~ 会补 =magpie/= 前缀，因为 Pi 把第一个斜杠之前当 provider）。

三处覆盖值得单独记：~+agent-shell-dot-subdir~ 把会话数据收敛到 =var/agent-shell/= 下，目录名由
项目目录名与 ~cwd~ 的 sha1 前 10 位拼成；~+agent-shell-resume-codex-fork~ 在 codex 的
=session/fork= 之后补一次 =session/resume=（不补的话事件订阅丢了、历史会被重放），它挂在
~agent-shell--send-request~ 上、深度 -50，好让 ~agent-shell-btw~ 的 advice 先看到 fork id；
~agent-shell--update-bootstrapping-fragment~ 被 ~:override~ 成 ~ignore~（不要那段引导文字）。
header 里另加了可点的选项：~+agent-shell-extend-header-model~ 把 ~collaboration_mode~ 与
~fast-mode~ 渲染成 header 按钮。

键位上 ~M-<return>~ 留给 ~agent-shell-submit-override~——它 steer 正在跑的 turn，普通换行留给
shell-maker 的 ~S-<return>~，两者不能互换。

+ 包名（GitHub xenodium/agent-shell）：~agent-shell~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/xenodium/agent-shell>
+ 官方手册：<https://20y.hu/~slink/journal/agent-shell/index.html>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package agent-shell
  :straight (:type git :host github :repo "xenodium/agent-shell")
  :require-incrementally
  (acp-traffic acp comint shell shell-maker transient
               agent-shell-faces agent-shell-work-buffer agent-shell-project
               agent-shell-config agent-shell-markdown agent-shell-diff
               agent-shell-styles agent-shell-usage agent-shell-list-edit
               agent-shell-viewport agent-shell-ui t)
  :bind (("C-c g a" . agent-shell)
         ("C-c g p" . agent-shell-prompt-compose)
         ("C-c g w" . agent-shell-send-dwim)
         :map agent-shell-mode-map
         ;; Leave M-<return> on `agent-shell-submit-override`, which steers a
         ;; busy turn.  A plain newline stays on shell-maker's S-<return>.
         ("C-c C-h" . agent-shell-help-menu)
         ("C-c C-q" . agent-shell-prompt-queue)
         ("C-c C-e" . agent-shell-prompt-steer))
  :custom-face
  (agent-shell-section-heading ((t (:inherit font-lock-function-name-face :height 0.9))))
  (agent-shell-section-annotation ((t (:inherit shadow :height 0.8))))
  :preface
  (defun +agent-shell-dot-subdir (subdir)
    "Return the centralized Agent Shell data directory for SUBDIR."
    (let* ((cwd (directory-file-name (agent-shell-cwd)))
           (name (file-name-nondirectory cwd))
           (slug (replace-regexp-in-string
                  "[^[:alnum:]._-]+" "-" (if (string-empty-p name) "root" name)))
           (project-key
            (format "%s-%s" slug (substring (secure-hash 'sha1 cwd) 0 10))))
      (expand-file-name
       (file-name-concat project-key ".agent-shell" subdir)
       (locate-user-emacs-file "var/agent-shell/"))))

  (cl-defun +agent-shell-resume-codex-fork
      (original &rest args &key state client request buffer on-success on-failure
                &allow-other-keys)
    "Resume Codex forks before completing their initialization.
This restores the event subscription without replaying history."
    (when (and (equal (map-elt request :method) "session/fork")
               (eq (map-nested-elt state '(:agent-config :identifier)) 'codex)
               (map-elt state :supports-session-resume))
      (setf (plist-get args :on-success)
            (lambda (fork-response)
              (let ((id (map-elt fork-response 'sessionId))
                    (params (map-elt request :params)))
                (unless (and (stringp id) (not (string-empty-p id))
                             (not (equal id (map-elt params 'sessionId))))
                  (error "Invalid fork session ID"))
                (agent-shell--send-request
                 :state state :client client :buffer (or buffer (map-elt state :buffer))
                 :request (acp-make-session-resume-request
                           :session-id id :cwd (map-elt params 'cwd)
                           :mcp-servers (map-elt params 'mcpServers)
                           :meta (map-elt params '_meta))
                 :on-success (lambda (resume-response)
                               (funcall on-success
                                        (map-merge 'alist fork-response resume-response
                                                   `((sessionId . ,id)))))
                 :on-failure on-failure)))))
    (apply original args))
  :init
  (defun +agent-shell-pi-model-id (model)
    "Prefix MODEL with magpie/ unless it already names that provider.
Pi treats the text before the first slash as the provider, so
`workbuddy/auto' must be sent as `magpie/workbuddy/auto'."
    (let ((name (if (symbolp model) (symbol-name model) model)))
      (if (string-prefix-p "magpie/" name)
          name
        (concat "magpie/" name))))

  (defun +agent-shell-pi-magpie-model-id ()
    "Return Magpie's current picker model for a new Pi session."
    (+agent-shell-pi-model-id (+gptel-magpie-model)))

  (defun +agent-shell-pi-make-agent-config ()
    "Pi config that follows Magpie's gateway picker."
    (let ((config (agent-shell-pi-make-agent-config)))
      (setf (alist-get :default-model-id config)
            #'+agent-shell-pi-magpie-model-id)
      config))

  (setq agent-shell-agent-configs '(+agent-shell-pi-make-agent-config)
        agent-shell-preferred-agent-config 'pi
        agent-shell-context-sources nil
        agent-shell-mcp-servers nil
        agent-shell-session-restore-verbosity 'full
        agent-shell-show-welcome-message nil
        agent-shell-chat-mode-enabled nil
        agent-shell-header-style 'text
        agent-shell-activity-group-expand-by-default 'latest
        agent-shell-markdown-table-zebra-stripe nil
        agent-shell-dot-subdir-function #'+agent-shell-dot-subdir
        agent-shell-show-context-usage-indicator 'detailed
        agent-shell-file-display-action '((display-buffer-reuse-window display-buffer-pop-up-window)))
  :config
  (defun +agent-shell-header-option (option shell-buffer)
    "Render OPTION with a menu that updates SHELL-BUFFER's session."
    (let* ((id (map-elt option :id))
           (current (map-elt option :current-value))
           (name (map-elt option :name))
           (label (agent-shell--config-option-value-name option current))
           (menu (make-sparse-keymap name))
           (map (make-sparse-keymap)))
      (dolist (choice (reverse (agent-shell--config-option-value-choices option)))
        (let ((value (map-elt (cdr choice) :value)))
          (define-key menu (vector (intern (format "value-%s" value)))
                      `(menu-item ,(car choice)
                                  (lambda () (interactive)
                                    (agent-shell-set-config-option-value
                                     :shell-buffer ,shell-buffer :id ,id :value ,value))
                                  :button (:toggle . ,(equal value current))))))
      (define-key map [header-line down-mouse-1] #'ignore)
      (define-key map [header-line mouse-1] menu)
      (propertize (if (equal id "fast-mode") (concat "Fast " label) label)
                  'face (pcase current
                          ("on" 'success)
                          ("off" 'shadow)
                          ("plan" 'warning)
                          (_ 'font-lock-constant-face))
                  'help-echo (format "%s: %s" name label)
                  'mouse-face 'mode-line-highlight
                  'local-map map)))

  (defadvice! +agent-shell-extend-header-model (original state &rest args)
    :around #'agent-shell--make-header-model
    "Append clickable collaboration and Fast mode options to the header."
    (let* ((model (apply original state args))
           (parts
            (cons (map-elt model :context-indicator)
                  (cl-loop for option in (agent-shell--config-options state)
                           when (member (map-elt option :id) '("collaboration_mode" "fast-mode"))
                           collect (+agent-shell-header-option option (current-buffer))))))
      (setf (map-elt model :context-indicator)
            (when-let* ((parts (delq nil parts)))
              (string-join parts " · ")))
      model))

  (advice-add #'agent-shell--update-bootstrapping-fragment :override #'ignore)
  ;; Run outside agent-shell-btw's request advice: it records the fork ID
  ;; before resume, allowing its normal close hook to clean up after failures.
  (advice-add #'agent-shell--send-request :around
              #'+agent-shell-resume-codex-fork '((depth . -50))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-ai.el= section for the ~agent-shell~ extensions

五个都是同一批邻仓包，共同点是都不改 ~agent-shell~ 核心，只往里挂 hook、键位或 advice。
~agent-shell-fork-tree~ README：浏览相关会话，包括装这个包之前就创建过的 fork；用通用的 ACP
=session/list=、=session/load= 与原生 =session/fork=，不读 provider 数据库或私有 parent 元数据。
~agent-shell-btw~ README（中文）：在侧栏里开临时 fork，创建时继承主会话上下文，之后可以连续对话、
执行工具；关闭时 hook 自动取消任务并删除临时会话记录。~agent-shell-links~ README：存指向会话的稳定
指针（session id + agent 标识 + 工作目录），打开时优先复用活着的 buffer，否则恢复会话——它本身只定义
函数，要自己调 setup。~agent-shell-tramp~ README：把 TRAMP 相关行为放在核心之外，让 ACP agent 在远端
TRAMP buffer 里跑，transcript 存在本地。~agent-shell-attention~ README：跟踪哪些 agent buffer 在等
输入，在 mode line 显示一个紧凑指示（~AS:n~），busy 但未 pending 的不计数。

各自的接线就一句话：fork-tree 关掉自动 rebuild、在三处键位图里绑 ~C-c g t~；btw 到处绑 ~C-c b~；
links 调 ~agent-shell-links-bookmark-setup~ 并在 ~ol~ 加载后注册 ~agent-shell:~ 链接类型；tramp 把
transcript 目录改到 =var/agent-shell/remote-transcripts/= 后开 mode；attention 等到第一次进入
~agent-shell-mode~ 才启 global mode，并明确不要零计数、不发系统通知。

+ 包名（GitHub roife/agent-shell-fork-tree）：~agent-shell-fork-tree~
+ 包名（GitHub roife/agent-shell-btw）：~agent-shell-btw~
+ 包名（GitHub ultronozm/agent-shell-links.el）：~agent-shell-links~
+ 包名（GitHub junyi-hou/agent-shell-tramp）：~agent-shell-tramp~

*** The =init-emacs-ai.el= section for ~agent-recall~

README：~agent-shell~ 会自动把完整对话 transcript 存成 Markdown 或 Org 文件（在项目的
=.agent-shell/transcripts/= 下，或另指定目录），久了就是一座知识库却没法搜；~agent-recall~ 给它们建
持久索引、做全文检索，并能从任一 transcript 恢复过去的会话。

~:init~ 那一组设置定的是检索范围与排序：四个搜索根（本配置仓库、==/src=、=/.config~、
=var/agent-shell=）、深度 3、用 ~consult-ripgrep~ 搜、按修改时间倒序、恢复时接着同一份 transcript；
~agent-shell-mode~ hook 上挂 ~agent-recall-track-sessions~ 记 session id。
~agent-recall-consult~ 是同一个仓库里的 consult 前端，这里只绑 =C-c g /= 并把
~agent-recall-consult-resumable-only~ 设成 ~nil~——搜索结果里也保留不能恢复的条目，方便只读查历史。

+ 包名（GitHub mrx-xo/agent-recall）：~agent-recall~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/mrx-xo/agent-recall>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package agent-recall
  :straight (:type git :host github :repo "mrx-xo/agent-recall")
  :hook (agent-shell-mode . agent-recall-track-sessions)
  :bind (("C-c g h" . agent-recall-browse)
         ("C-c g u" . agent-recall-resume))
  :init
  (setq agent-recall-search-paths
        (list user-emacs-directory
              (expand-file-name "src" "~")
              (expand-file-name "~/.config")
              (expand-file-name "var/agent-shell" user-emacs-directory))
        agent-recall-max-depth 3
        agent-recall-search-function 'consult-ripgrep
        agent-recall-browse-sort 'modified-desc
        agent-recall-resume-continue-transcript t))
#+end_src

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package agent-recall-consult
  :bind ("C-c g /" . agent-recall-consult-search)
  :init
  (setq agent-recall-consult-resumable-only nil))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-ai.el= section for ~agent-review~

README：用一个 agent 实现、再让另一个 agent 评审——自动收集 git 改动发给选定的 agent，结果进一个可
跳转的列表，能按严重性分类，也能把修复发回实现 agent。

本配置自己加了一个入口 ~+agent-review-grok~：绑在 ~C-c g r~，先 ~require~ ~agent-shell-xai~，再用它的
grok config 调 ~agent-review~——于是「评审者」固定成 Grok Build，与被评审的 agent 是谁无关。

+ 包名（GitHub nineluj/agent-review）：~agent-review~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/nineluj/agent-review>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
(use-package agent-review
  :straight (:type git :host github :repo "nineluj/agent-review")
  :commands agent-review
  :bind ("C-c g r" . +agent-review-grok)
  :preface
  (defun +agent-review-grok ()
    "Review staged and unstaged Git changes with Grok Build."
    (interactive)
    (require 'agent-review)
    (require 'agent-shell-xai)
    (agent-review (agent-shell-xai-make-grok-config))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-ai.el= section for ~gptel-copilot~ (disabled)

只有 recipe：=roife/gptel-copilot= 本地没有克隆，因此也没有 README 可引，这里只写 recipe 与用法意图，
不描述它的行为。

从 ~use-package~ 的写法看，它是 ~gptel~ 之上的 inline 补全：~prog-mode~ 下开
~gptel-copilot-mode~，~C-e~ / ~M-f~ 被换成「有候选就接受、没有就退回原本的移动命令」；backend 是
OpenAI OAuth（~gptel-openai-oauth~），与本章主用的 Magpie 网关是两套凭据。
~:disabled~ 就是因为它现在只有 OAuth 那套凭据可用；留着是因为那两个命令展示了「补全没有候选时退回
原本的移动命令」这个写法，删掉就只剩 git 历史可查。

+ 包名（GitHub roife/gptel-copilot）：~gptel-copilot~
+ Git 仓库：
  - GitHub：<https://github.com/roife/gptel-copilot>

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
;; [gptel-copilot] gptel-powered inline code completion
(use-package gptel-copilot
  :disabled
  :straight (:type git :host github :repo "roife/gptel-copilot")
  :commands gptel-copilot-mode
  :preface
  (defun +gptel-copilot-complete ()
    "Accept the completion, or move to the end of code or line."
    (interactive)
    (or (gptel-copilot-accept-completion)
        (mwim-end-of-code-or-line)))

  (defun +gptel-copilot-complete-word ()
    "Accept one completion word, or move forward one word."
    (interactive)
    (or (gptel-copilot-accept-completion-by-word 1)
        (forward-word)))

  :hook (prog-mode . gptel-copilot-mode)
  :bind (:map gptel-copilot-mode-map
              ("C-e" . +gptel-copilot-complete)
              ("M-f" . +gptel-copilot-complete-word))
  :config
  (require 'gptel-openai-oauth)

  (setq gptel-copilot-model 'gpt-5.4-mini
        gptel-copilot-idle-delay 0.2
        gptel-copilot-backend (gptel-make-openai-oauth "OpenAI OAuth Inline"
                                :request-params '(:reasoning (:effort "low")))))
#+end_src

*** The =init-emacs-ai.el= section to provide the feature

~provide~ 把 ~init-emacs-ai~ 登记成 feature，这个模块的加载到此结束。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp :tangle core/init-emacs-ai.el :mkdirp yes
(provide 'init-emacs-ai)
#+end_src

* The custom libraries of this configuration

除了 =core/init-emacs-*.el= 这些配置模块，本配置里还有一层「自己的库」：宏与函数。它们不是
第三方包，也不打算发布，但被多个模块共用，改动的影响面比单个模块大。

** ~add-hook!~ 与 ~defadvice!~

两个宏定义在 ~init-emacs-util~，是全文的基础设施：

- ~add-hook!~ 一次给多个 hook 装一批函数，支持 ~:local~、~:append~、~:depth~、~:remove~、
  ~:call-immediately~、~:unless-daemonp-call-immediately~ 等关键字。后者用于「非 daemon 启动时
  立即执行一次」的场景（例如加载主题），daemon 下则推迟到有 frame 时。
- ~defadvice!~ 把 ~advice-add~ 写成声明式，形式和 ~use-package~ 的 ~:config~ 接近，避免在模块里
  散落 ~advice-add~ / ~advice-remove~ 的成对调用。

** 以 ~+~ 为前缀的函数

配置私有的函数一律以 ~+~ 开头（~+load-theme~、~+elfeed-display-entry~、~+mu4e-apply~、
~+agent-shell-dot-subdir~ 等），与上游包的命名区分。它们分散在各自模块里，不集中到一个文件——
函数跟着使用它的模块走，读代码时不必在两个文件之间跳。

~init-emacs-util~ 里还有几个被多处调用的 helper（~+unfill-region~、
~+enable-conservative-scrolling~），命名同样是 ~+~ 前缀。

** 模块内的小宏

有些模块在本地定义小宏（例如 ~init-emacs-completion~ 里的
~+corfu-move-to-minibuffer~、~init-emacs-web~ 里的 ~+elfeed-*~）。原则是：只被一个模块用的，
就留在那个模块；被两个以上模块用的，才提到 ~init-emacs-util~。

* Frequently Asked Questions (FAQ)

** 为什么很多模块，而不是一个 init.el？

一个模块对应一类关注点（主题、补全、Org、邮件……），改一个领域只读一个文件。更重要的是加载顺序：
顺序依赖集中写在 =init.el= 的 ~require~ 列表里，而不是散落在各文件内部。代价是文件多，收益是
「谁在什么时候加载」这件事始终只有一个答案。

** 为什么每个包都显式写 ~:straight~？

~straight-use-package-by-default~ 设为 ~nil~。如果默认接管，任何一个 ~use-package~ 都会去装包，
读代码时无法区分「这个包由 straight 管理」和「这个包是内置的」。显式写出来，代价是多打几个字。

** 加一个新包要改几处？

只有一处：在对应模块里加一个 ~use-package~（带 ~:straight~），然后 ~C-c C-v C-t~ 重新 tangle。
不需要改 =init.el=，也不需要手动装包——straight 在下次启动时按 ~:straight~ 声明安装。

** 为什么主题在 ~init-emacs-theme~，而不在 ~init-emacs-ui~？

因为平台层（~init-emacs-platform~）在加载时就会触发外观变化回调，它需要一个已经定义好的
~+load-theme~；而 ~init-emacs-theme~ 排在平台层之前。把主题相关的东西放进 ui 会让这条依赖边
反过来。

** 改了配置但没生效怎么办？

先确认改的是 =emacs-config.org=，然后 ~C-c C-v C-t~ 重新 tangle，再重启 Emacs。只在运行中的
Emacs 里 ~eval-buffer~ 往往不够：~custom-set-faces~、~defcustom~ 这类「设置」不会因为删掉配置
而撤销，需要重启或显式重置。

** 为什么文档改动也会让 =core/= 出现 diff？

不会。散文不参与 tangle；如果只改散文却看到 =core/= 有 diff，说明误动了 ~#+begin_src~ 块。
正常情况应当满足 =git diff core/= 为空。

** 怎么排查启动变慢？

=init.el= 开头挂了一个 ~window-setup-hook~，启动后会在 echo area 打印 window-setup 与 after-init
的耗时。要更细的分解，用 ~emacs --init-directory ... -Q --batch~ 之外的 ~use-package~ 计时，
或临时打开 ~init-file-debug~ 看 =early-init.el= 里被抑制的那些告警。
